📊 需背：生词 591 个 · 句型表达 17 条（已统一归入背词计划）
💡 常犯错误（定性）
  • 拼写填空：complex→compose、comprehension、hierarchies、terrestrial、adaptation、construction、remarkable、feat、pioneered、innovative 等高频词反复拼错
  • 句子插入题：This/it 回指抓不住（Sleep 题选了 Location 2，正解 Location 1）
  • 段落关系题：第3段是「解决第2段问题」还是「反驳」，功能判断错（Sports 题选 contradicts，正解 addresses）
  • 词汇题：cohesion=团结(unity) 误选 awareness；anticipate=预期 误选 avoid；deviate=differ 误选 distract（词根没拆对）
  • 绝对化词陷阱：eliminate(完全免除) 当 reduce(减少)，三次踩坑
🎯 下一步建议
① 拼写是最大失分点，先去背「✍️阅读填词题」专项 84 词（错词都在里面）。② 句子插入题抓回指代词 This/it/the latter，指代前一句刚提的内容。③ 段落关系题看段落功能（解决/反驳/举例/引入），别只看字面。④ 词汇题拆词根：co(一起)+her(粘)=cohesion、de(离)+via(路)=deviate、anti(预先)+cip(拿)=anticipate。

❌ 错题/错空 · 95 处 · 重点看
❌[2026-09-01] The Erie Canal 伊利运河 · 空1你写 constructure → 正确 construction
拼写：construction(建设)=construct+ion。construct 不是 constructure
❌[2026-09-01] The Erie Canal 伊利运河 · 空2你写 remarcable → 正确 remarkable
拼写：remarkable(非凡的)=re+mark+able。remark 不是 remarc
❌[2026-09-01] The Erie Canal 伊利运河 · 空3你写 feel → 正确 feat
近形词听混：feat(壮举) 听成 feel。feat=功绩，feet/fit/feat 易混
❌[2026-09-01] The Erie Canal 伊利运河 · 空4你写 pionercal → 正确 pioneered
拼写：pioneered(开创)=pioneer+ed。pioneer 不是 pionercal
❌[2026-09-01] The Erie Canal 伊利运河 · 空5你写 innovation → 正确 innovative
词形错：innovative(创新的，形容词) 写成 innovation(创新，名词)。题要形容词
❌[2026-09-01] Sports Social Integration 体育与社会融合 · 题1你选 B → 正确 C
词汇题陷阱：你选了 B(awareness)——cohesion=co+her(粘在一起)=团结(unity→C)。词根 her 是『粘』
❌[2026-09-01] Sports Social Integration 体育与社会融合 · 题2你选 B → 正确 D
细节题陷阱：你选了 B(规则太严)——原文是 favoritism or exclusionary practices(偏袒或排斥→D)，不是 strict about rules
❌[2026-09-01] Sports Social Integration 体育与社会融合 · 题3你选 B → 正确 A
段落关系题陷阱：你选了 B(反驳)——第3段是 address(解决)第2段的挑战(A)，不是 contradicts。注意段落功能
❌[2026-09-01] Social Structures 社会结构 · 空1你写 compose → 正确 complex
近形词听混：complex(复杂的) 写成 compose(作曲)。com-plex=com+plex(折叠)
❌[2026-09-01] Social Structures 社会结构 · 空2你写 terre______ → 正确 terrestrial
拼写：terrestrial(陆地的)=terra(地)+strial。terra=地(同 territory)
❌[2026-09-01] Social Structures 社会结构 · 空3你写 comprehention → 正确 comprehension
拼写：comprehension(理解)=com+prehend(抓)+sion。中间是 prehend 不是 prehent
❌[2026-09-01] Social Structures 社会结构 · 空4你写 hierachical → 正确 hierarchies
拼写：hierarchies(等级制度) 复数。hier-archy，中间是 hierarch 不是 hierach
❌[2026-09-01] Social Structures 社会结构 · 空5你写 adapotions → 正确 adaptation
拼写：adaptation(适应)=adapt+ation。adapt 不是 adopt
❌[2026-09-01] Sleep And Memory 睡眠与记忆 · 题2你选 B → 正确 A
句子插入题陷阱：你选了 B——'This interpretation' 的 This 要回指前一句刚提的解读(neural data management)，应在 Location 1(A)。抓回指代词 This
❌[2026-09-01] Color and Pigment 色彩颜料 · 空1你写 understand → 正确 understood
时态：have long understood(现在完成，用过去分词 understood)
❌[2026-09-01] Color and Pigment 色彩颜料 · 空2你写 utilately → 正确 utilizing
拼写：utilizing(利用)=utilize+ing。utilize 不是 utilate
❌[2026-09-01] Color and Pigment 色彩颜料 · 空3你写 is → 正确 to
近形词听混：to 听成 is
❌[2026-09-01] Color and Pigment 色彩颜料 · 空4你写 emphasiz → 正确 emphasis
词形：emphasis(强调，名词)=emphas+is。emphasize 是动词
❌[2026-09-01] Color and Pigment 色彩颜料 · 空5你写 evole → 正确 evoke
近形词听混：evoke(唤起) 写成 evole。e-voke=唤出(同 vocal 词根)
❌[2026-09-01] College Craft Event 校园手工艺 · 题1你选 B → 正确 C
主旨题陷阱：你选了 B(推广社团)——原文是告知 Craft-A-Thon 聚会(C)，没提建立 crafting club
❌[2026-08-29] Viral Marketing · 题3你选 B → 正确 D

❌[2026-08-29] Urban Green Spaces · 题2你选 A → 正确 C
修辞目的题陷阱：你选了 A(地价贵)——parking lots 是举例「可改造的闲置地」(repurpose unused lots such as...)，服务于『解决办法』这个论点，不是解释地价。such as 后面是举例
❌[2026-08-29] Podcast Project · 题1你选 A → 正确 D
态度题陷阱：你选了 A(希望她听播客)——原文 'didn't show up...second time' 是负面态度(annoyed 恼火)，不是 hopeful。抓 didn't show up/second time 的负面信号
❌[2026-08-29] John Cage & Prepared Piano · 题3你选 A → 正确 D

❌[2026-08-29] Environmental Club · 题1你选 A → 正确 B

❌[2026-08-29] Bookstore Training · 题1你选 C → 正确 A

❌[2026-08-29] Bookstore Training · 题2你选 C → 正确 A

❌[2026-08-29] Bookstore Training · 题3你选 C → 正确 A

❌[2026-08-28] Understanding Mental States · 空1你写 fundational → 正确 fundamental
拼写错：fundamental(根本的)=fundament(基础)+al。你写 fundational，把 -ment 漏成 -tion。记 funda-MENT-al，中间是 ment(心智)不是 tion
❌[2026-08-28] The Power of Placebos · 题1你选 C → 正确 B

❌[2026-08-28] The Art of Conducting · 题3你选 D → 正确 B

❌[2026-08-28] Salamander Symbiosis · 题1你选 C → 正确 D

❌[2026-08-28] Medieval Town Origins · 题4你选 B → 正确 A

❌[2026-08-28] Green Roof Benefits · 题1你选 A → 正确 C
细节题陷阱：你选了 A(免除取暖空调)——eliminate(完全免除)是绝对化词，原文是 'reduces the need'(减少需求)。绝对化词=陷阱信号
❌[2026-08-28] Geological Formations · 空1你写 layout → 正确 layers

❌[2026-08-28] Geological Formations · 空2你写 preduce → 正确 pressed
拼写错：pressed(被压实)=press+ed。你写 preduce 是乱拼。记 press 压→pressed
❌[2026-08-28] Geological Formations · 空3你写 fully → 正确 fused
近形词听混：fused(熔合)=fuse(熔)。你写 fully(完全)。pressed and fused=压实+熔合
❌[2026-08-28] Economics And Resource Allocation · 空1你写 examinat → 正确 examines
漏了 -es 结尾：examining→examines，第三·人称单数要加 -s（他/it）。你写成 examinat 连 -es 都没写全
❌[2026-08-28] Economics And Resource Allocation · 空2你写 indivicials → 正确 individuals
拼写中间段错：indi-vi-du-als，你写成 indivicials。记口诀：in-di-vi-du-al（把 vid 想成 video 的『看』）
❌[2026-08-28] Economics And Resource Allocation · 空3你写 businesman → 正确 businesses
既拼错又混词：business（生意）≠ businessman（商人）。复数 businesses 直接 business + es，你把 busi-ness 拼成 busines，又串到 man
❌[2026-08-28] Economics And Resource Allocation · 空4你写 resolves → 正确 resource
近形词听错/看错：resource（资源）= re+source（来源）。你写成 resolves（解决），是听成了 resolve 的变形。resource 结尾 -source 是『源头』
❌[2026-08-28] Economics And Resource Allocation · 空5你写 afford → 正确 affect
近形词混淆：affect（影响，动词）= af+fect。你写成 afford（负担得起）。口诀：affect 影响 effect 结果；afford 是『出得起钱』，两个 a 开头的动词易混
❌[2026-08-28] Earbud Product · 题1你选 C → 正确 B

❌[2026-08-28] Decalcomania · 题3你选 A → 正确 C

❌[2026-08-28] Decalcomania · 题4你选 C → 正确 B
细节题陷阱：你选了 C(超现实主义流行)——题问 Ernst『特别欣赏』什么，原文是 lack of control(无法控制)，不是『谁让它流行』。appreciate 对应 lack of control

❌[2026-08-28] Campus International Festival · 题2你选 C → 正确 D

❌[2026-08-28] Campus Announcement · 题1你选 A → 正确 B
主旨题陷阱：你选了 A(课程表)——原文开头是 'set our clocks one hour ahead'(拨快一小时)=时间变化 B，不是课程表。通知类第一个信息点往往是主旨
❌[2026-08-28] Behavioral Economics · 题2你选 D → 正确 B
词汇题陷阱：你选了 D(distracts)——deviate 和 distract 都带『离』，但 deviate 是偏离标准(differ)，distract 是转移注意力(分心)。deviate=de+via(路)；distract=dis+tract(拉)
❌[2026-08-28] Art Museum Exhibition · 题2你选 D → 正确 C
细节题陷阱：你选了 D(买新画)——'Refreshment sales will support cultural programs' 是茶点销售的用途，跟『入场费』无关。题问 entrance fees，原文 free admission=免费 C
❌[2026-08-28] Apartment Maintenance · 题2你选 C → 正确 D
时间计算题陷阱：题目问『几点能回来』，选项里放『完工时间 12:00』做干扰——必须把修饰语『one hour after』也算进去
❌[2026-08-28] Antimicrobial Resistance Challenges · 题2你选 B → 正确 A
句子题陷阱：你选了 B(药厂开发慢)——但题问『AMR 如何发生/耐药机制』，答案 A(细菌通过基因突变+基因共享产生耐药)。B 讲的是经济问题，不是耐药机制
❌[2026-08-28] Antimicrobial Resistance Challenges · 题3你选 A → 正确 B
细节题陷阱：你选了 A(保险公司)——原文说的是『lower financial returns 回报低』，对应 B(less profitable 利润更低)。A 的『insurance』原文根本没提
❌[2026-08-28] Antimicrobial Resistance Challenges · 题4你选 A → 正确 C
词汇题陷阱：comprehensive 你选了 A(understandable 可理解)——词根 com(一起)+prehend(抓住)=全抓住=全面、包罗万象 → C(thorough)。不是『可理解』
❌[2026-08-28] Antimicrobial Resistance Challenges · 题5你选 B → 正确 A
EXCEPT题陷阱：你选了 B(加强处方监管)——但这是原文明确提的策略。EXCEPT 要选『原文没提的』= A(增加预防感染的投资)。你又选了『提到的』
❌[2026-08-28] Ancient Fossils · 空1你写 advantatical → 正确 advantageous
拼写错：advantage（优势）+ -ous（形容词后缀）→ advantageous（有利的）。你写成 advantatical，是自造后缀。记：advantage 去 e 加 -ous
❌[2026-08-21] 学术阅读 2 · 题1你选 B → 正确 C
字面撞词但非作者意图：B 靠句中并列的 hybrid 和 electric vehicles 两个词诱你选『对比』，但 and 是并列不是比较，原文没比较优劣。D 是『范围错位』——把后段才讲的『困难』误当成这段的目的。修辞目的题要看『这句服务上文哪个论点』，不是看句子表面有哪些词。
❌[2026-08-21] 学术阅读 2 · 题5你选 B → 正确 C
『相关就往前塞』的方位陷阱：B 与待插句内容最相关，容易误以为要放在它旁边、且顺手放到『前面』；但待插句是对 [B] 句结论的数据展开，必须放其『后面』（即 [C]）。插入题口诀：先判待插句是『展开/举证/数据』还是『新论点』，若是展开就放被展开句之后；再用连接词与指代（这里 whereas 承接 compared to other energy technologies）复核衔接。
❌[2026-08-21] 学术阅读 1 · 题5你选 C → 正确 B
字面撞词但方向读反：C 里的 recent findings / animal behavior 都是原文露过脸的词，勾着你去选；但作者对 assumptions 的态度是 challenge（推翻），C 却说『被支持』，是把态度方向读反了的经典陷阱。另有 A 的『偷换程度』（挑战≠已抛弃）和 D 的『偷换主语/答非所问』。
❌[2026-08-21] 填词 3 · 空1你写 final → 正确 field
该填 field（领域）。回原句看：This ___ encompasses...（这个领域涵盖…），this ___ 指代上句的 oceanography，语义是『这个学科/领域』＝field。你写的 final 是听辨错：field /fiːld/ 元音是 /iː/，final /ˈfaɪnl/ 元音是 /aɪ/，两个差很远。听不准时用语义反查——this field（这个领域）是学术文章固定说法，final 在这讲不通。
❌[2026-08-21] 填词 3 · 空4你写 currency → 正确 currents
该填 currents（洋流，复数）。回原句看：the exploration of ocean ___（对洋流的探索），ocean currents 是固定搭配。你写的 currency 是听辨＋词义双错：currency 是货币，跟海洋毫无关系；两词只是开头音近，尾音不同（/ˈkɜːrənts/ vs /ˈkɜːrənsi/）。听到海洋语境里的 /ˈkɜːrənts/ 别脑补成货币，靠上下文锁定 currents。
❌[2026-08-21] 填词 3 · 空8你写 ues → 正确 use
该填 use（使用）。回原句看：Oceanographers ___ satellites...（海洋学家使用卫星…），主语后要谓语动词 use。你写的 ues 是手误——字母顺序写反了（u-s-e 写成 u-e-s）。词本身你听对了，纯粹是打字顺序错。对策：短词写完回扫一眼，确认字母顺序对。
❌[2026-08-21] 填词 2 · 空4你写 make → 正确 made
该填过去式 made。回原句看：Cooking food... ___ digesting nutrients much easier，这是主句谓语（主语是 Cooking food），且全文时态是过去式（was used / provided），所以用 made，不是原形 make。判断：先确认它是主句谓语动词，再套全文过去时＝加 -ed 或用不规则过去式（make→made）。
❌[2026-08-21] 填词 2 · 空5你写 nutriente → 正确 nutrients
该填复数名词 nutrients /ˈnuːtriənts/（营养物质）。回原句看：digesting ___（消化营养物质），digesting 后面要名词作宾语，且泛指营养物质用复数。你写的 nutriente 是拼写错——没有这个词，词尾是 -ent＋复数 -s＝nutrients，别加多余的 e。
❌[2026-08-21] 填词 2 · 空6你写 easily → 正确 easier
该填比较级 easier。回原句看：made digesting nutrients much ___（让消化容易得多）。信号词 much 只能修饰比较级（much easier＝容易得多），所以要 easy 的比较级 easier，不是副词 easily。你写 easily 是把词性判错了：这里 made + 宾语 + 形容词补语（easier），要形容词/比较级，不要副词。记死：much/far/a lot + 比较级。
❌[2026-08-21] 填词 2 · 空9你写 coling → 正确 colder
该填比较级 colder。回原句看：in ___ climates（在较冷的气候里），暗含与温暖处对比，用 cold 的比较级 colder。你写的 coling 是拼写＋词形双错：既不是真词，也没做成比较级。判断：cold→colder（单音节形容词直接加 -er），听到 /ˈkoʊldər/ 的 -er 尾就是比较级。
❌[2026-08-21] 填词 1 · 空3你写 be → 正确 by
该填介词 by。回原句看：These incidents can be caused ___ varying factors，caused BY（被…引起）是被动语态的固定搭配（be + 过去分词 + by + 施动者）。你写的 be 是动词，这里前面已有 be caused，不可能再来一个 be——一看到 be caused/be made 后接施动者，就是 by。
❌[2026-08-21] 填词 1 · 空7你写 lose → 正确 loss
该填名词 loss。回原句看：environmental changes, habitat ___, and catastrophic events 是三个并列名词短语，habitat ___ 要一个名词跟 changes/events 平行，所以是 loss（栖息地丧失）。你写的 lose 是动词——名动混了：名词 loss（-ss 结尾，重音同 loss），动词 lose（-se 结尾，读 /luːz/）。并列结构里全是名词，别塞动词。
❌[2026-08-21] 填词 1 · 空8你写 catast…（没拼完） → 正确 catastrophic
该填形容词 catastrophic /ˌkætəˈstrɑːfɪk/（灾难性的），修饰后面的 events。回原句看：catastrophic events 是形-名搭配。你只拼了 catast… 没拼完——这是拼写关：记词根 cata(向下)+strophe(转)，四音节 cat-a-STROPH-ic，重音在第三节，务必把 -strophic 整段拼全。名词是 catastrophe（重音在第二节），别混。
❌[2026-08-21] 填词 1 · 空9你写 Studenly → 正确 Studying
该填动名词 Studying。回原句看：Studying extinctions helps scientists...，Studying extinctions（研究灭绝）是整句主语，句首动词要用 -ing 动名词形式才能当主语。你写的 Studenly 是听辨+拼写双错：/ˈstʌdiɪŋ/ 听成了别的，且拼出了不存在的词。抓住『句首＋后面紧跟谓语 helps』这个信号，就知道要动名词做主语。
❌[2026-08-21] 填词 1 · 空10你写 he…（没拼完） → 正确 helps
该填 helps（单三）。回原句看：主语 Studying extinctions 是动名词短语，视作单数第三人称，谓语动词加 -s，所以 helps 不是 help。你只写了 he… 没写完。判断口诀：动名词/不定式做主语＝单数＝谓语用单三 -s。

🚦 信号词高亮（听不懂时靠这些画骨架）：转折/对比因果/结论举例/定义列举/递进强调

📖 阅读模块 · 全部题目积累

该模块所有历史题目的跨场聚合（46 篇任务）。
🎧 完全听不懂时·边听边抓（尤其学术讲座）——尽量多理解的 5 步
① 开头一句定主题：学术讲座第一句几乎必点主题——"Today we'll focus on X" / "Let's talk about X"。抓住 X 是什么，后面全围着它转。细节没听懂，至少知道在讲什么，主旨题就能拿下。
② 顺信号词拼骨架（配合上面🚦分色高亮）：
  • 🔴 but/however/instead → 前面被转折/否定，后面才是重点
  • 🔵 because/so/therefore → 因果，抓「因为什么 → 所以什么」
  • 🟢 for example/such as/called → 要举例或下定义了，例子帮你理解前面的抽象点；"a X called Y" 必考 Y 是什么
  • 🟣 first/then/also/another → 在列点，心里数 1、2、3
  • 🟠 the key/importantly/actually → 划重点，这句最可能考
③ 抓实词、放虚词：名词+动词是内容，介词冠词不用抠。某句没听懂就别卡住——等下一个信号词/例子，它常会换句话再解释一遍。
④ 重复 = 重点：反复出现的词（stalagmite、algal bloom…）就是核心概念，围着它记；数字/人名先记下，但「为什么提它」比它本身更重要。
⑤ 学术讲座万能骨架：现象/定义 → 原因/机制 → 例子/证据 → 影响/结论。就算只抓到「讲某现象、有原因、举了例、最后有个启示」，也够答对主旨题 + 修辞目的题 + 推断题（占大头）。
✓[2026-09-01] The Erie Canal 伊利运河（4/10 ❌错5）4/10
📖 原文 · 点任意词展开满配卡 · 🔊 读全文
The Erie Canal, completed in 1825, is a historic waterway in the United States that linked the Atlantic Ocean to the Great Lakes. Its construction was a remarkable engineering feat and pioneered many innovative techniques. The canal significantly reduced transportation costs and time from New York City to the interior of the nation, propelling the city into a major commercial hub. The success of the Erie Canal inspired the building of other canals and infrastructure projects across the United States.
🖊 逐空对照 · ✍️ 正确答案已遮住，先回忆再点开核对
#你写的正确考点
1constructure✗ construction拼写：construction(建设)=construct+ion。construct 不是 constructure
2remarcable✗ remarkable拼写：remarkable(非凡的)=re+mark+able。remark 不是 remarc
3feel✗ feat近形词听混：feat(壮举) 听成 feel。feat=功绩，feet/fit/feat 易混
4pionercal✗ pioneered拼写：pioneered(开创)=pioneer+ed。pioneer 不是 pionercal
5innovation✗ innovative词形错：innovative(创新的，形容词) 写成 innovation(创新，名词)。题要形容词
6✓ The
7✓ significantly
8✓ transportation
9✓ and
10✓ from
🔴 你的漏洞：5错：construction/remarkable/feat/pioneered/innovative 都拼错或词形错
🛠 补强：词根：struct(建)→construct/construction；pioneer(开拓者)→pioneered；feat=壮举(同 feature 词根)
✓[2026-09-01] Sports Social Integration 体育与社会融合（3/5 ❌错2）3/5
📖 原文 · 点任意词展开满配卡 · 🔊 读全文
Sports can promote social integration by bridging gaps between people of various backgrounds. Participation in sports can lead to increased social cohesion and improved relationships among diverse groups. For example, community soccer leagues often bring together people from different ethnicities, creating an environment where cultural differences are celebrated and mutual respect is cultivated. However, the impact of sports on social integration is not without challenges. Competitive environments can sometimes exacerbate social tensions, particularly when favoritism or exclusionary practices are present. Despite these issues, many initiatives aim to use sports as a platform for social change. One such initiative is the Sport for Peace program, which focuses on conflict resolution through team-building activities and collaborative sports events. The role of sports in social integration extends beyond participation. Spectatorship and fandom also play significant roles in uniting people. Major international sports events like the Olympics or the World Cup serve as opportunities for nations to come together and share common goals.
词汇题The word 'cohesion' is closest in meaning to ❌ 你答错了
A. diversity
B. awareness
C. unity
D. celebration
🙈 先自己判断，再点开看答案 & 老师精讲
❌ 你错在哪：词汇题陷阱：你选了 B(awareness)——cohesion=co+her(粘在一起)=团结(unity→C)。词根 her 是『粘』
A. diversity
B. awareness ✗ 你选的
C. unity ✓ 正确
D. celebration
📍 定位'increased social cohesion and improved relationships'
🧠 推理cohesion = 凝聚力、团结。词根 co(一起)+her(粘)=粘在一起 → unity → C
✂️ 排除B(awareness 意识)你选了——cohesion 是『团结』不是『意识』
细节题In which situation can sports have an especially negative impact? ❌ 你答错了
A. When people from different ethnicities are brought together
B. When participants are unreasonably strict about rules
C. When the social environment is not favorable to competition
D. When social tensions are heightened by favoring or excluding certain participants
🙈 先自己判断，再点开看答案 & 老师精讲
❌ 你错在哪：细节题陷阱：你选了 B(规则太严)——原文是 favoritism or exclusionary practices(偏袒或排斥→D)，不是 strict about rules
A. When people from different ethnicities are brought together
B. When participants are unreasonably strict about rules ✗ 你选的
C. When the social environment is not favorable to competition
D. When social tensions are heightened by favoring or excluding certain participants ✓ 正确
📍 定位'exacerbate social tensions, particularly when favoritism or exclusionary practices are present'
🧠 推理负面影响场景=favoritism(偏袒)或 exclusion(排斥) → D
✂️ 排除B(对规则过于严格)原文是 favoritism/exclusion 不是 rule strict
段落关系题What is the relationship between paragraphs 2 and 3? ❌ 你答错了
A. Paragraph 3 discusses ways of addressing a problem mentioned in paragraph 2
B. Paragraph 3 contradicts a theory proposed in paragraph 2
C. Paragraph 3 provides evidence for an idea in paragraph 2
D. Paragraph 3 introduces a new topic
🙈 先自己判断，再点开看答案 & 老师精讲
❌ 你错在哪：段落关系题陷阱：你选了 B(反驳)——第3段是 address(解决)第2段的挑战(A)，不是 contradicts。注意段落功能
A. Paragraph 3 discusses ways of addressing a problem mentioned in paragraph 2 ✓ 正确
B. Paragraph 3 contradicts a theory proposed in paragraph 2 ✗ 你选的
C. Paragraph 3 provides evidence for an idea in paragraph 2
D. Paragraph 3 introduces a new topic
📍 定位第2段：sports 也有挑战(favoritism/exclusion)。第3段：Sport for Peace 等 initiative 用体育解决冲突
🧠 推理第3段是『解决第2段提到的问题』→ A
✂️ 排除B(contradicts 矛盾)你选了——第3段不是反驳，是应对/解决
🔴 你的漏洞：题1词汇cohesion、题2细节、题3段落关系都错
🛠 补强：cohesion=团结(unity)。段落关系题看『第3段是解决还是反驳第2段』
✓[2026-09-01] Social Structures 社会结构（5/10 ❌错5）5/10
📖 原文 · 点任意词展开满配卡 · 🔊 读全文
Understanding the complex social structures and behaviors exhibited by terrestrial mammals is crucial for both conservation efforts and enhancing our comprehension of evolutionary processes. Many mammals, such as elephants and primates, display intricate communication systems and social hierarchies that facilitate cooperation and survival. These actions are not merely instinctive; they are dynamic responses to environmental pressures, showcasing adaptability and intelligence. By studying these animals, researchers can gain insights into the fundamental mechanisms behind social organization and behavioral adaptation.
🖊 逐空对照 · ✍️ 正确答案已遮住，先回忆再点开核对
#你写的正确考点
1compose✗ complex近形词听混：complex(复杂的) 写成 compose(作曲)。com-plex=com+plex(折叠)
2terre______✗ terrestrial拼写：terrestrial(陆地的)=terra(地)+strial。terra=地(同 territory)
3comprehention✗ comprehension拼写：comprehension(理解)=com+prehend(抓)+sion。中间是 prehend 不是 prehent
4hierachical✗ hierarchies拼写：hierarchies(等级制度) 复数。hier-archy，中间是 hierarch 不是 hierach
5adapotions✗ adaptation拼写：adaptation(适应)=adapt+ation。adapt 不是 adopt
6✓ structures
7✓ conservation
8✓ communication
9✓ environmental
10✓ fundamental
🔴 你的漏洞：5错：complex→compose、comprehension、hierarchies、terrestrial、adaptation 都拼错
🛠 补强：词根串背：terra(地)→terrestrial/territory；prehend(抓)→comprehension；hier(神圣)+archy(统治)→hierarchy
✓[2026-09-01] Sleep And Memory 睡眠与记忆（4/5 ❌错1）4/5
📖 原文 · 点任意词展开满配卡 · 🔊 读全文
Sleep is not merely a passive period of rest; it plays an active role in the consolidation of memory, a process that has been extensively examined by neuroscientists over several decades. During sleep, distinct stages—most notably rapid-eye-movement (REM) sleep and deep sleep—contribute in different ways to how memories are stored and organized. Experimental research suggests that while REM sleep strengthens emotionally charged memories, deep sleep supports the retention of factual knowledge and the acquisition of skills. Some researchers further propose that sleep functions as a kind of neural 'data management' system. [A] While awake, the brain continuously forms new connections as it processes incoming information, creating a dense and potentially inefficient network. [B] During sleep, however, unnecessary connections are trimmed, whereas essential ones are reinforced, [C] a reorganization that enhances the brain's ability to retrieve information accurately. [D] Not surprisingly, sleep deprivation has been shown to impair memory retention and conceptual integration. Even a single night of lost sleep can reduce recall and interfere with learning. Nevertheless, studies also indicate that recovery sleep—particularly when it is deep and uninterrupted—can partially reverse these effects, suggesting that although sleep is critical for memory, the brain retains a limited capacity to adapt to disruption.

A. To suggest that the effects of sleep deprivation follow logically from earlier claims
B. To emphasize that people are often conscious of memory lapses
C. To point out a reason for scientists' interest in sleep-memory link
D. To acknowledge that sleep quality is as essential as quantity

C. To point out a reason for scientists' interest in sleep-memory link
D. To acknowledge that sleep quality is as essential as quantity

🧠 推理Not surprisingly = 顺理成章，睡眠剥夺损伤记忆是从前文逻辑推出 → A

✓[2026-09-01] Color and Pigment 色彩颜料（5/10 ❌错5）5/10
📖 原文 · 点任意词展开满配卡 · 🔊 读全文
Throughout history, color and pigment have played a central role in art, shaping how we perceive and interpret visual experiences. Artists have long understood the emotional and psychological power of color, utilizing it to convey moods, create emphasis, and evoke responses. For example, red often symbolizes passion, while blue can elicit calmness. These symbolic associations can vary across cultures and time periods, allowing artists to communicate deeper layers of meaning through their color choices.
🖊 逐空对照 · ✍️ 正确答案已遮住，先回忆再点开核对
#你写的正确考点
1understand✗ understood时态：have long understood(现在完成，用过去分词 understood)
2utilately✗ utilizing拼写：utilizing(利用)=utilize+ing。utilize 不是 utilate
3is✗ to近形词听混：to 听成 is
4emphasiz✗ emphasis词形：emphasis(强调，名词)=emphas+is。emphasize 是动词
5evole✗ evoke近形词听混：evoke(唤起) 写成 evole。e-voke=唤出(同 vocal 词根)
6✓ have
7✓ emotional
8✓ psychological
9✓ of
10✓ moods
🔴 你的漏洞：5错：understood时态、utilizing拼写、to听成is、emphasis词形、evoke拼写
🛠 补强：词根：voc(声音)→evoke/vocal；utilize=use 的高级版。时态 have+过去分词

✓[2026-08-29] Viral Marketing · 病毒式营销（3/4 ❌错1）3/4⏱ 3m11s
🎧 听力原文 · 点词展开 ·
Today we're diving into the dynamic and often unpredictable world of viral marketing. Viral marketing is a strategy that has redefined how brands connect with audiences in the digital age. Viral marketing harnesses the power of social networks, relying on individuals to share content that resonates, entertains, or provokes thought. When executed effectively, a viral campaign can generate massive exposure and amplify brand visibility at a fraction of the cost of traditional advertising methods. You don't need to pay for ads when the video or meme is spreading on its own. What makes viral marketing compelling is its potential for exponential reach. A single post, video, or meme can cascade through online communities, crossing geographic and demographic boundaries in mere hours. But this strategy is far from foolproof. One of the biggest challenges is unpredictability. There is no guaranteed formula for virality. What resonates with one audience may be ignored by another, and content that is edgy or controversial may be more likely to go viral, but it can also spark backlash and damage a company's reputation. Another risk is loss of control. Once content goes viral, it is no longer in the hands of the brand. Memes, remixes, and commentary can distort the original message, sometimes in ways that conflict with the brand's values or goals. Despite these risks, viral marketing remains an invaluable tool, particularly for startups and emerging brands seeking rapid traction. Next, let's talk about some case studies of particularly effective viral marketing campaigns.
🇨🇳 听力原文翻译（先自己读，点开中英对照）
今天，我们将深入病毒式营销这个充满活力又常常难以预测的世界。病毒式营销是一种重新定义了数字时代品牌与受众建立联系方式的策略。病毒式营销利用社交网络的力量，依靠个人来分享那些能引起共鸣、娱乐大众或引发思考的内容。当执行得当时，一场病毒式营销活动能够带来巨大的曝光度，并以远低于传统广告方法的成本放大品牌知名度。当视频或表情包自行传播时，你就不需要为广告付费。病毒式营销之所以吸引人，在于其指数级传播的潜力。一条帖子、视频或表情包就能层层扩散到各个网络社区，在短短数小时内跨越地域和人群界限。但这种策略远非万无一失。最大的挑战之一就是不可预测性。病毒式传播没有万无一失的公式。能引起一批受众共鸣的内容，可能被另一批受众忽视；前卫或有争议的内容可能更容易走红，但也可能引发反弹、损害公司声誉。另一个风险是失去控制。一旦内容走红，它就不再掌握在品牌手中。表情包、二次混剪和评论可能扭曲原始信息，有时会以与品牌价值观或目标相冲突的方式扭曲。尽管存在这些风险，病毒式营销仍然是一种宝贵的工具，尤其是对寻求快速立足的初创公司和新兴品牌而言。接下来，我们来谈一些特别有效的病毒式营销案例。

When executed effectively, a viral campaigncan generatemassive exposureand amplifybrand visibilityat a fraction of the cost of traditional advertising methods.
先自己拆，再点开看直译 + 读法

🧭 怎么读懂：主干 a viral campaign can generate massive exposure and amplify brand visibility（一场活动能带来巨大曝光并放大品牌知名度），其中 and 连接两个并列动宾结构。句首 When executed effectively 是省略主语的条件状语（当被有效执行时）；句末 at a fraction of the cost of... 是介词短语作状语，强调成本之低。

✓[2026-08-29] Urban Green Spaces · 城市绿地（3/4 ❌错1）3/4⏱ 3m33s
🎧 听力原文 · 点词展开 ·
Today, we're revisiting the topic of urban green spaces, green areas in cities like parks and gardens. These spaces are known to improve air quality and reduce heat in cities. They also support the health of city residents. Creating and maintaining urban green spaces can be challenging, however, especially in densely populated cities. An obvious issue is space. In areas where land is expensive or already developed, finding room for parks or gardens can be difficult. One way cities address this is by building green roofs. That's a layer of vegetation planted on top of a building. Green roofs help cool buildings, absorb rainwater, and add greenery without taking up ground space. Cities also often repurpose unused lots, such as abandoned parking areas or vacant lots, turning them into small parks or gardens. Vertical gardens are another innovation. These are walls covered with plants, often using special structures that allow greenery to grow upward. They're useful in places where horizontal space is limited and can improve air quality and insulation. While cost and upkeep of green spaces are legitimate concerns, many cities are adopting green space quotas, policies that require a minimum amount of greenery in new developments. These quotas mandate builders to include features such as trees, rooftop gardens, or courtyards. Though they may raise construction costs, urban planners support them because they ensure that green space is not overlooked in city growth. Quotas help balance development with environmental health and are a step toward more sustainable urban living.
🇨🇳 听力原文翻译（先自己读，点开中英对照）
今天，我们来重访城市绿地这个话题，也就是城市里像公园和花园这样的绿色区域。人们认为这些空间能够改善空气质量、降低城市温度。它们也有益于城市居民的健康。然而，创建和维护城市绿地可能充满挑战，尤其是在人口密集的城市里。一个显而易见的问题是空间。在土地昂贵或已经开发完毕的地区，为公园或花园腾出空间可能很困难。城市解决这一问题的一个办法是建造绿色屋顶。那是在建筑物顶部种植的一层植被。绿色屋顶有助于给建筑降温、吸收雨水，并且在不占用地面空间的情况下增加绿化。城市还经常改造闲置地块，例如废弃的停车场或空地，把它们变成小公园或花园。垂直花园是另一项创新。这些是覆盖着植物的墙，通常利用特殊结构让绿植向上生长。它们在水平空间有限的地方很有用，并且能够改善空气质量和保温隔热。虽然绿地的成本和维护是合理的顾虑，但许多城市正在采用绿地配额制，也就是要求新开发项目具备最低绿化量的政策。这些配额强制开发商纳入树木、屋顶花园或庭院等设施。尽管这些措施可能抬高建筑成本，城市规划者仍然支持它们，因为它们能确保绿地不会在城市发展中被忽视。配额有助于在发展与环保健康之间取得平衡，是迈向更可持续城市生活的一步。

主语谓语宾语从句介词/修饰状语

直译：在土地昂贵或已开发的地区，为公园或花园找地方是困难的。
🧭 怎么读懂：先剥离开头介词短语 In areas，其内部 where land is expensive or already developed 是定语从句修饰 areas，说明'什么样的地区'。主句主语是动名词短语 finding room for parks or gardens，谓语 can be，表语 difficult。整体=状语+主系表，抓住 finding...can be difficult 这个主干即可。

先自己拆，再点开看直译 + 读法

📝 关键句 · 点词展开卡 · 学表达
Today, we're revisiting the topic of urban green spaces, green areas in cities like parks and gardens.
今天，我们来重访城市绿地这个话题，也就是城市里像公园和花园这样的绿色区域。
🔑 开篇信号词 Today we're revisiting 点明主题；逗号后 green areas in cities like parks and gardens 是对 green spaces 的同位解释，定义题常考。
These spaces are known to improve air quality and reduce heat in cities.
这些空间被认为能够改善空气质量并降低城市温度。
🔑 are known to 引出公认功能；并列 improve air quality + reduce heat，两项信息，易考双选。
They also support the health of city residents.
它们也有益于城市居民的健康。
🔑 also 递进信号，补充第三个功能（居民健康）。
Creating and maintaining urban green spaces can be challenging, however, especially in densely populated cities.
然而，创建和维护城市绿地可能充满挑战，尤其是在人口密集的城市。
🔑 however 转折信号，话题由好处转向难点；especially 强调限定条件（人口密集城市）。
An obvious issue is space.
一个显而易见的问题是空间。
🔑 An obvious issue 主题句，点出第一个难点=空间。
In areas where land is expensive or already developed, finding room for parks or gardens can be difficult.
在土地昂贵或已经开发完毕的地区，为公园或花园腾出空间可能很困难。
🔑 where 定语从句解释哪些地区难找地；expensive or already developed 并列两个原因。
One way cities address this is by building green roofs.
城市解决这一问题的一个办法是建造绿色屋顶。
🔑 One way...is... 结构信号，提出第一个解决方案（绿色屋顶）。
That's a layer of vegetation planted on top of a building.
那是在建筑物顶部种植的一层植被。
🔑 That's... 对 green roofs 下定义。
Green roofs help cool buildings, absorb rainwater, and add greenery without taking up ground space.
绿色屋顶有助于给建筑降温、吸收雨水，并在不占用地面空间的情况下增加绿化。
🔑 并列三个动词 cool/absorb/add，易考细节多选；without taking up ground space 强调不占地。
Cities also often repurpose unused lots, such as abandoned parking areas or vacant lots, turning them into small parks or gardens.
城市还经常改造闲置地块，例如废弃停车场或空地，把它们变成小公园或花园。
🔑 also 列举信号，第二个方案；such as 举例 abandoned parking areas / vacant lots。
Vertical gardens are another innovation.
垂直花园是另一项创新。
🔑 another 列举信号，第三个方案（垂直花园）。
These are walls covered with plants, often using special structures that allow greenery to grow upward.
这些是覆盖着植物的墙，通常利用特殊结构让绿植向上生长。
🔑 定义垂直花园；often using 现在分词补充说明结构；that 从句解释结构作用。
They're useful in places where horizontal space is limited and can improve air quality and insulation.
它们在水平空间有限的地方很有用，并且能改善空气质量和保温隔热。
🔑 where 限定适用场景；and 连接两个作用（空气质量+隔热）。
While cost and upkeep of green spaces are legitimate concerns, many cities are adopting green space quotas, policies that require a minimum amount of greenery in new developments.
虽然绿地的成本和维护是合理的顾虑，但许多城市正在采用绿地配额制，也就是要求新开发项目具备最低绿化量的政策。
🔑 While 让步信号，先承认成本担忧；主句 many cities are adopting green space quotas；policies that... 同位语解释 quota。
These quotas mandate builders to include features such as trees, rooftop gardens, or courtyards.
这些配额强制开发商纳入树木、屋顶花园或庭院等设施。
🔑 mandate 讲配额的具体要求；such as 举例 trees/rooftop gardens/courtyards。
Though they may raise construction costs, urban planners support them because they ensure that green space is not overlooked in city growth.
尽管它们可能抬高建筑成本，城市规划者仍然支持它们，因为它们能确保绿地不会在城市发展中被忽视。
🔑 Though 让步（承认提高成本）；because 因果，解释规划者支持的原因。
Quotas help balance development with environmental health and are a step toward more sustainable urban living.
配额有助于在发展与环保健康之间取得平衡，是迈向更可持续城市生活的一步。
🔑 总结句，两个并列作用（balance + a step toward），尾句常考主旨。
🔴 你的漏洞：题2修辞目的错——parking lots 是举例『可改造地块』，你选了『地价贵』
🛠 补强：修辞目的题看 such as/for example 后面是举例，服务于『解决空间问题的办法』这个论点。城市绿地词串：green roofs/vertical gardens/repurpose/quotas/vegetation

✓[2026-08-29] John Cage & Prepared Piano · 预制钢琴（2/4 ❌错2）2/4⏱ 3m24s
🎧 听力原文 · 点词展开 ·
Imagine taking a regular piano and transforming its sound by inserting everyday objects like screws, bolts, and rubber erasers between the strings. This is what's known as a prepared piano. It was developed by the avant garde composer John Cage in 1940. Cage was working at a university in Seattle, and he needed to write music with percussion to accompany dancers. But the performance space's stage wasn't big enough for percussion instruments like drums. So he made the sounds with just the piano by placing objects between the strings. Now, Cage spent time searching for items that made the kind of sounds he was looking for. It had a feel of improvisation. Cage himself said that looking for items was kind of like walking on the beach and looking for shiny or beautiful seashells that you liked. That is, he didn't know ahead of time what items he wanted to use. He just kept looking around and seeing what suited his needs. Cage ended up composing a collection of music for prepared piano in the mid 1940s. He included specific instructions for how to prepare the piano. For this collection, Cage was inspired by ancient Hindu theory and about eight essential emotions. He wanted his listeners to sense eight essential emotions from Hindu philosophy. The result is a blending of sounds that would be familiar to a Western audience, plus sounds reminiscent of Indonesian gamelan. Gamelan is traditional Indonesian percussion music. This prepared piano music really raised John Cage's profile in the music world.
🇨🇳 听力原文翻译（先自己读，点开中英对照）
想象一下，把一架普通的钢琴，通过在琴弦之间塞入螺丝、螺栓和橡皮擦等日常物品来改变它的声音。这就是所谓的'预制钢琴'。它由先锋派作曲家约翰·凯奇于1940年发明。凯奇当时在西雅图的一所大学工作，他需要创作带打击乐的音乐来为舞者伴奏。但演出场地的舞台不够大，放不下鼓之类的打击乐器。所以他只用钢琴，通过在琴弦之间放置物品来制造这些声音。接着，凯奇花时间寻找能发出他想要的那种声音的物品。这带有一种即兴创作的感觉。凯奇本人说过，找物品有点像在海滩上散步，寻找你喜欢的闪亮或美丽的海贝壳。也就是说，他事先并不知道自己要用哪些物品。他只是不停地四处观察，看什么适合他的需要。凯奇最终在20世纪40年代中期创作了一套预制钢琴音乐合集。他附上了如何'预制'钢琴的具体说明。在这套作品里，凯奇受到古印度理论和'八种基本情绪'的启发。他希望自己的听众能感受到来自印度哲学的八种基本情绪。其成果是既融合了西方听众熟悉的音响，又加入了让人联想到印度尼西亚甘美兰的音响。甘美兰是印度尼西亚传统的打击乐。这些预制钢琴音乐确实提升了约翰·凯奇在音乐界的知名度。
🔬 长难句精拆 · 点彩块看成分 · 点词展开卡
主语谓语宾语从句介词/修饰状语
Imaginetaking a regular piano and transforming its soundby inserting everyday objects like screws, bolts, and rubber erasers between the strings.
先自己拆，再点开看直译 + 读法
直译：想象一下，把一架普通钢琴通过在琴弦之间塞入螺丝、螺栓、橡皮擦等日常物品来改变它的声音。
🧭 怎么读懂：祈使句，主语 you 省略，谓语 Imagine（想象）。宾语是 taking...and transforming... 两个并列的动名词短语。其后 by inserting...between the strings 是介词短语，说明改变声音的方式（通过往琴弦间塞东西）。
Cage himselfsaidthat looking for items was kind of like walking on the beach and looking for shiny or beautiful seashells that you liked.
先自己拆，再点开看直译 + 读法
直译：凯奇本人说，找物品有点像在海滩上散步，寻找你喜欢的闪亮或美丽的海贝壳。
🧭 怎么读懂：主干 Cage himself said that...。that 引导的宾语从句里，主语是动名词 looking for items，谓语 was kind of like，后面 walking on the beach and looking for...seashells 是两个并列动名词作 like 的宾语；句末 that you liked 是定语从句修饰 seashells。整体是'A像B'的比喻结构。

📝 关键句 · 点词展开卡 · 学表达
Imagine taking a regular piano and transforming its sound by inserting everyday objects like screws, bolts, and rubber erasers between the strings.
想象一下，把一架普通钢琴通过在琴弦之间塞入螺丝、螺栓和橡皮擦等日常物品来改变它的声音。
🔑 Imagine... 祈使句画面化引入；inserting...between the strings 说明操作方式，like 举例。
This is what's known as a prepared piano.
这就是所谓的'预制钢琴'。
🔑 what's known as 引出术语定义 prepared piano。
It was developed by the avant garde composer John Cage in 1940.
它由先锋派作曲家约翰·凯奇于1940年发明。
🔑 developed by...in 1940 人物+时间，易考细节。
Cage was working at a university in Seattle, and he needed to write music with percussion to accompany dancers.
凯奇当时在西雅图的一所大学工作，他需要创作带打击乐的音乐来为舞者伴奏。
🔑 was working...and he needed to 交代背景与动机（为舞蹈写打击乐）。
But the performance space's stage wasn't big enough for percussion instruments like drums.
但演出场地的舞台不够大，放不下鼓之类的打击乐器。
🔑 But 转折信号，转折点=舞台太小放不下鼓。
So he made the sounds with just the piano by placing objects between the strings.
所以他只用钢琴，通过在琴弦之间放置物品来制造这些声音。
🔑 So 因果信号，引出解决方案=只用钢琴塞东西。
Now, Cage spent time searching for items that made the kind of sounds he was looking for.
接着，凯奇花时间寻找能发出他想要的那种声音的物品。
🔑 Now 转接信号，进入寻找物品的过程；that 从句说明找什么声音。
It had a feel of improvisation.
这带有一种即兴创作的感觉。
🔑 a feel of improvisation 概括寻找过程的即兴感。
Cage himself said that looking for items was kind of like walking on the beach and looking for shiny or beautiful seashells that you liked.
凯奇本人说过，找物品有点像在海滩上散步，寻找你喜欢的闪亮或美丽的海贝壳。
🔑 Cage himself said that... 引述本人比喻（海边捡贝壳），易考。
That is, he didn't know ahead of time what items he wanted to use.
也就是说，他事先并不知道自己要用哪些物品。
🔑 That is 解释说明信号，重述=事先不知道要什么。
He just kept looking around and seeing what suited his needs.
他只是不停地四处观察，看什么适合他的需要。
🔑 just kept looking 强调过程随意，无预设。
Cage ended up composing a collection of music for prepared piano in the mid 1940s.
凯奇最终在20世纪40年代中期创作了一套预制钢琴音乐合集。
🔑 ended up 结果信号，1940年代中期完成一套作品。
He included specific instructions for how to prepare the piano.
他附上了如何'预制'钢琴的具体说明。
🔑 specific instructions 附具体说明，细节点。
For this collection, Cage was inspired by ancient Hindu theory and about eight essential emotions.
在这套作品里，凯奇受到古印度理论和'八种基本情绪'的启发。
🔑 was inspired by 灵感来源=古印度理论+八种情绪。
He wanted his listeners to sense eight essential emotions from Hindu philosophy.
他希望自己的听众能感受到来自印度哲学的八种基本情绪。
🔑 wanted his listeners to sense 目的=让听众感受八种情绪。
The result is a blending of sounds that would be familiar to a Western audience, plus sounds reminiscent of Indonesian gamelan.
其成果是既融合了西方听众熟悉的音响，又加入了让人联想到印度尼西亚甘美兰的音响。
🔑 The result is a blending of...plus... 成果=西方熟悉声+印尼甘美兰，注意混合点。
Gamelan is traditional Indonesian percussion music.
甘美兰是印度尼西亚传统的打击乐。
🔑 定义 gamelan，术语解释。
This prepared piano music really raised John Cage's profile in the music world.
这些预制钢琴音乐确实提升了约翰·凯奇在音乐界的知名度。
🔑 really raised...profile 总结其地位提升，尾句主旨。
🔴 你的漏洞：题3修辞目的错——seashells 是比喻 Cage 找材料的『随性方式』，你当成了字面『用的物品』
🛠 补强：修辞目的题抓比喻义：like walking on the beach looking for seashells = 随性/即兴地找材料(approach)，不是字面物品。音乐词串：prepared piano/percussion/improvisation/gamelan/reminiscent

Welcome, trainees.

🔑 So...start by 引出第一项培训内容=上架。

库存放在后面的房间，你们的经理会告诉你们每天由谁负责补货上架。

有时货物在运输途中到达时会有损坏。
🔑 Sometimes...arrives damaged 情形说明（运输损坏）。

只需告知经理，但不要把损坏的商品摆上架。
🔑 Just let...but don't put out 指令+转折，强调不要摆损坏品。
Our goal is for customers to have the best experience possible.

🔑 Our goal is for... 总结目标=最佳顾客体验。

🛠 补强：通知/告示类：抓 'now that.../because.../our goal is...' 这些『原因+目的』信号。别被 surface 词(inventory/sale)带偏，题问 why 就找原因句
✓[2026-08-28] Understanding Mental States · 心理状态理解（9/10 ❌错1）9/10
📖 原文 · 点任意词展开满配卡 · 🔊 读全文
One of the topics psychologists investigate is our capacity to understand the mental states of others. This ability is fundamental for social interaction because it enables us to predict and explain the behavior of others. Is this skill innate or shaped by experience? Is understanding others' mental states a matter of simulation (imagining ourselves in their place) or theory (applying a set of rules or concepts)? These inquiries contribute to broader discussions about human cognition.
🇨🇳 全文翻译（先自己读，点开中英对照）
心理学家研究的话题之一，是我们理解他人心理状态的能力。这种能力对社会交往至关重要，因为它使我们能够预测和解释他人的行为。这种技能是与生俱来的，还是由经验塑造的？理解他人的心理状态，靠的是模拟（想象自己置身于他们的处境），还是靠理论（应用一套规则或概念）？这些探究促成了关于人类认知的更广泛讨论。
🔬 长难句精拆 · 点彩块看成分 · 点词展开卡
主语谓语宾语从句介词/修饰状语
Isunderstanding others' mental statesa matter of simulation(imagining ourselves in their place)or theory(applying a set of rules or concepts)
先自己拆，再点开看直译 + 读法
直译：理解他人的心理状态，靠的是模拟（想象自己处在他们的位置上），还是靠理论（应用一套规则或概念）？
🧭 怎么读懂：这是一个一般疑问句，主干是 Is [understanding...] a matter of simulation or theory?。主语是动名词短语 understanding others' mental states（理解他人心理状态），系动词 is 提前构成疑问，表语是 a matter of simulation...or theory。两个括号里的 imagining... 和 applying... 分别是 simulation 和 theory 的同位解释。

✓[2026-08-28] The Power of Placebos · 安慰剂效应（4/5 ❌错1）4/5
📖 原文 · 点任意词展开满配卡 · 🔊 读全文
The placebo effect challenges conventional ideas about medical treatment efficacy. What is a placebo? A patient's condition sometimes improves if they believe that they are receiving an effective treatment, even if they are in fact receiving a sugar pill. In this example, the sugar pill, which has no active ingredient, is a placebo. Surprisingly, this effect can sometimes rival real medications in clinical trials.
Recent studies suggest that the placebo effect might be rooted in the brain's complex chemistry. When a patient anticipates positive effects, their brain can release chemicals like endorphins, which may cause genuine physiological changes. In one study, patients receiving placebo "painkillers" reported pain relief comparable to the one produced by actual painkillers, highlighting the brain's powerful role. Some researchers propose using the placebo effect deliberately to enhance therapy outcomes, arguing that the mind-body connection should be tapped into more.
However, ethical considerations arise: deceiving patients could undermine trust in healthcare. For example, even when a placebo leads to measurable improvement, knowingly administering it without full disclosure may violate principles of informed consent, raising questions about autonomy and transparency in patient care. This delicate balance between harnessing the placebo effect and maintaining ethical standards continues to be a topic of debate.
🇨🇳 全文翻译（先自己读，点开中英对照）
安慰剂效应挑战了关于医学治疗有效性的传统观念。什么是安慰剂？当患者相信自己正在接受有效治疗时，病情有时会改善，即使他们实际上服用的是一颗糖丸。在这个例子中，这颗不含任何活性成分的糖丸，就是安慰剂。令人惊讶的是，这种效应有时在临床试验中能与真正的药物相媲美。 最近的研究表明，安慰剂效应可能根植于大脑复杂的化学机制之中。当患者预期会产生积极效果时，他们的大脑会释放内啡肽等化学物质，从而可能引发真实的生理变化。在一项研究中，服用安慰剂"止痛药"的患者报告了与实际止痛药相当程度的疼痛缓解，凸显了大脑的强大作用。一些研究人员提议有意使用安慰剂效应来增强治疗效果，认为应当更充分地利用身心之间的联系。 然而，伦理问题随之而来：欺骗患者可能会破坏医患之间的信任。例如，即使安慰剂带来了可衡量的改善，在未充分告知的情况下有意使用它，也可能违反知情同意的原则，引发关于患者自主权和医疗透明度的质疑。在利用安慰剂效应与维持伦理标准之间取得微妙平衡，这始终是一个争论不休的话题。
🔬 长难句精拆 · 点彩块看成分 · 点词展开卡
主语谓语宾语从句介词/修饰状语
For example, a quick upward flickmight indicatea sudden crescendo(gradual increase in volume), while a gentle circular motion could suggesta more legato (smooth and connected) passage
先自己拆，再点开看直译 + 读法
直译：例如，一个快速向上的挑动可能表示突发的渐强（音量逐渐增大），而一个轻柔的划圈动作则可能示意一段更连奏（流畅而连贯）的乐段。
🧭 怎么读懂：用 while 连接两个并列分句，形成对照：前半句 a quick upward flick might indicate a crescendo；后半句 a gentle circular motion could suggest a legato passage。括号里的内容分别是 crescendo 和 legato 的解释。先看 while 把句子分成对比的两半。
While the conductor's role during performances might appear spontaneous, itis groundedin meticulous preparation
先自己拆，再点开看直译 + 读法
直译：虽然指挥家在演出中的角色看似即兴，但它建立在精心准备的基础之上。
🧭 怎么读懂：While 引导让步状语从句（虽然……看似即兴），主句是 it is grounded in meticulous preparation。be grounded in 表示“以……为基础”。先认出 While=虽然而非“当……时”，再抓主句主语 it 指代 the conductor's role。

✓[2026-08-28] The Art of Conducting · 指挥艺术（4/5 ❌错1）4/5
📖 原文 · 点任意词展开满配卡 · 🔊 读全文
Orchestral conducting is often seen as a magical art form. With a baton and their hands, conductors shape an orchestra's performance. Their role is not just to keep time; they guide the interpretation and emotional delivery of a piece. This requires a deep understanding of music and the ability to inspire musicians.
One key aspect of conducting is communication. Conductors convey their intentions through gestures, facial expressions, and body language. This nonverbal communication is crucial during live performances. A slight baton movement can signal different tempo, dynamics, or mood. For example, a quick upward flick might indicate a sudden crescendo (gradual increase in volume), while a gentle circular motion could suggest a more legato (smooth and connected) passage.
Rehearsal technique is another important element. Conductors spend significant time with orchestras to fine-tune performances. This involves breaking down music, addressing challenges, and experimenting with interpretations. Rehearsals allow conductors to build a cohesive sound and align musicians with their vision.
While the conductor's role during performances might appear spontaneous, it is grounded in meticulous preparation. Conductors study scores in detail, learning every part of the orchestra's contribution. This understanding allows them to anticipate issues and react swiftly during concerts. Conductors often spend years refining their technique and adapting their style.
🇨🇳 全文翻译（先自己读，点开中英对照）
管弦乐指挥常常被视为一种神奇的艺术形式。指挥家凭借指挥棒和双手塑造乐团的演奏。他们的职责不仅仅是打拍子，还要引导一部作品的诠释和情感表达。这需要对音乐有深刻的理解，以及能够激励演奏者的能力。 指挥的一个关键方面是沟通。指挥家通过手势、面部表情和肢体语言传达意图。这种非言语沟通在现场演出中至关重要。一个细微的指挥棒动作就能示意不同的速度、力度或情绪。例如，一个快速向上的挑动可能表示突发的渐强（音量逐渐增大），而一个轻柔的划圈动作则可能示意一段更连奏（流畅而连贯）的乐段。 排练技巧是另一个重要因素。指挥家会花大量时间与乐团一起打磨演出。这包括拆解音乐、攻克难点以及尝试不同的诠释。排练让指挥家能够打造统一的声音，并使演奏者与其愿景保持一致。 虽然指挥家在演出中的角色看似即兴，但它建立在精心准备的基础之上。指挥家会仔细研读总谱，了解乐团每一部分的贡献。这种理解使他们能够预判问题并在音乐会上迅速应对。指挥家往往要花多年时间打磨技巧、调整风格。

主语谓语宾语从句介词/修饰状语

先自己拆，再点开看直译 + 读法
直译：例如，一个快速向上的挑动可能表示突发的渐强（音量逐渐增大），而一个轻柔的划圈动作则可能示意一段更连奏（流畅而连贯）的乐段。
🧭 怎么读懂：用 while 连接两个并列分句，形成对照：前半句 a quick upward flick might indicate a crescendo；后半句 a gentle circular motion could suggest a legato passage。括号里的内容分别是 crescendo 和 legato 的解释。先看 while 把句子分成对比的两半。
While the conductor's role during performances might appear spontaneous, itis groundedin meticulous preparation
先自己拆，再点开看直译 + 读法
直译：虽然指挥家在演出中的角色看似即兴，但它建立在精心准备的基础之上。
🧭 怎么读懂：While 引导让步状语从句（虽然……看似即兴），主句是 it is grounded in meticulous preparation。be grounded in 表示“以……为基础”。先认出 While=虽然而非“当……时”，再抓主句主语 it 指代 the conductor's role。
EXCEPT题All of the following are mentioned about communication in orchestral conducting EXCEPT（关于指挥的沟通，除了） ✅ 你答对了

B. It allows conductors to give instructions to musicians 让指挥给乐手指示

D. It sometimes takes the form of spoken instructions 有时是口头指示
🙈 先自己判断，再点开看答案 & 老师精讲

词汇题The word "anticipate" is closest in meaning to（anticipate 最接近） ❌ 你答错了

B. expect 预期

❌ 你错在哪：词汇题陷阱：anticipate 你选了 D(solve 解决)——词根 anti(预先)+cip(拿)=提前拿到=预料。anticipate issues 是『预见问题』不是『解决问题』，正确 B(expect 预期)
A. understand 理解
B. expect 预期 ✓ 正确
C. avoid 避免
D. solve 解决 ✗ 你选的
📍 定位'allows them to anticipate issues and react swiftly'（让他们能…问题并迅速反应）
🧠 推理anticipate = anti(前)+cip(拿)=提前拿到=预料。anticipate issues = 预见问题 → expect
✂️ 排除avoid(回避)语义相反——anticipate 是『提前知道』不是『躲开』；understand/solve 偏离

✓[2026-08-28] Salamander Symbiosis · 蝾螈与绿藻共生（1/4 ❌错3）1/4⏱ 2m50s
🎧 听力原文 · 点词展开 ·
We've been talking about symbiosis, which, to remind you, is a beneficial interaction between two different species. A good example is birds and seed producing plants. The birds eat the seeds the plants produce, and the plants benefit from birds dispersing those seeds. Okay, so here's a truly remarkable case of symbiosis: the relationship between the spotted salamander, a type of amphibian, and green algae, which are tiny plant like aquatic organisms. The partnership begins when the salamander lays its eggs in freshwater ponds. The salamander eggs are contained within a jelly like capsule, and the algae from the pond move in and form an integral part of this jelly like mass as the embryos develop. All the while, the algae are photosynthesizing, transforming sunlight into usable energy, a process that releases oxygen. This is critical because the embryos need oxygen, and pond water tends to be low in it. And in return, the algae get a nutrient that they need to survive: nitrogen. The embryos produce readily available nitrogen as a waste product, and the algae happily use it. And what's cool is we now know that the algae don't just live in the egg capsule; they can enter the embryos cells, meaning photosynthesis is occurring inside a vertebrate, a highly advanced animal, a phenomenon almost unheard of. Corals and a few other marine invertebrates do this, but among vertebrates it is extremely rare.
🇨🇳 听力原文翻译（先自己读，点开中英对照）
我们一直在讨论共生，提醒一下，共生是两种不同物种之间的互利互动。一个很好的例子是鸟类和结种子的植物。鸟类吃植物产出的种子，而植物则受益于鸟类传播这些种子。好，那么这里有一个真正了不起的共生案例：斑点钝口螈（一种两栖动物）和绿藻（微小的类植物水生生物）之间的关系。这种伙伴关系始于钝口螈在淡水池塘里产卵。螈卵被包裹在果冻状的卵囊里，随着胚胎发育，池塘里的藻类移入，成为这个果冻状团块的组成部分。自始至终，藻类都在进行光合作用，把阳光转化为可用的能量，这一过程会释放氧气。这一点至关重要，因为胚胎需要氧气，而池塘水往往含氧量很低。作为回报，藻类获得了一种它们生存所需的养分：氮。胚胎作为一种代谢废物，产生了现成可用的氮，而藻类乐意地加以利用。而最酷的是，我们现在知道藻类不仅仅生活在卵囊里；它们还能进入胚胎的细胞，也就是说，光合作用正在脊椎动物——一种高度进化的动物——体内进行，这几乎是一个闻所未闻的现象。珊瑚和一些其他海洋无脊椎动物会这么做，但在脊椎动物中，这种情况极其罕见。
🔬 长难句精拆 · 点彩块看成分 · 点词展开卡
主语谓语宾语从句介词/修饰状语
Okay, so here 's a truly remarkable case of symbiosis: the relationship between the spotted salamander, a type of amphibian, and green algae, which are tiny plant like aquatic organisms.
先自己拆，再点开看直译 + 读法
直译：好，那么这里有一个真正了不起的共生案例：斑点钝口螈（一种两栖动物）和绿藻（微小的类植物水生生物）之间的关系。
🧭 怎么读懂：主干是 here's a truly remarkable case。冒号后面 the relationship between A and B 是对 case 的具体解释：A 是 spotted salamander（中间 a type of amphibian 是同位语补充说明），B 是 green algae（which 引导定语从句修饰藻类）。听时只需锁定『蝾螈』和『绿藻』两个主角即可。
what's cool is we now know that the algae don't just live in the egg capsule; they can enter the embryos cells, meaning photosynthesis is occurring inside a vertebrate, a highly advanced animal, a phenomenon almost unheard of.
先自己拆，再点开看直译 + 读法
直译：而最酷的是，我们现在知道藻类不仅仅生活在卵囊里；它们还能进入胚胎的细胞，也就是说，光合作用正在脊椎动物——一种高度进化的动物——体内进行，这几乎是一个闻所未闻的现象。
🧭 怎么读懂：主干是 what's cool is...，what 引导主语从句。is 后面的表语从句是 we now know that...，that 从句里用分号连接两个并列分句：藻类不只是住在卵囊里，还能进入胚胎细胞。最后 meaning... 是非谓语补充说明光合作用在脊椎动物体内进行，末尾 a highly advanced animal 和 a phenomenon almost unheard of 是同位语追加强调。

📝 关键句 · 点词展开卡 · 学表达
We've been talking about symbiosis, which, to remind you, is a beneficial interaction between two different species.
我们一直在讨论共生，提醒一下，共生是两种不同物种之间的互利互动。
🔑 复习引入信号 We've been talking about；抓 symbiosis 的定义 beneficial interaction（互利互动）。
A good example is birds and seed producing plants.
一个很好的例子是鸟类和结种子的植物。
🔑 举例信号 A good example is；抓 birds 和 plants 两个例子主体。
The birds eat the seeds the plants produce, and the plants benefit from birds dispersing those seeds.
鸟类吃植物产出的种子，而植物则受益于鸟类传播这些种子。
🔑 双向互利结构；抓 eat the seeds / benefit from dispersing，注意两方各自的收获。
Okay, so here's a truly remarkable case of symbiosis: the relationship between the spotted salamander, a type of amphibian, and green algae, which are tiny plant like aquatic organisms.
好，那么这里有一个真正了不起的共生案例：斑点钝口螈（一种两栖动物）和绿藻（微小的类植物水生生物）之间的关系。
🔑 信号词 Okay 引入核心案例；抓两个主角 spotted salamander（斑点钝口螈）和 green algae（绿藻）。
The partnership begins when the salamander lays its eggs in freshwater ponds.
这种伙伴关系始于钝口螈在淡水池塘里产卵。
🔑 过程起点信号 begins when；抓 lays its eggs in freshwater ponds。
The salamander eggs are contained within a jelly like capsule, and the algae from the pond move in and form an integral part of this jelly like mass as the embryos develop.
螈卵被包裹在果冻状的卵囊里，随着胚胎发育，池塘里的藻类移入，成为这个果冻状团块的组成部分。
🔑 并列句描述第一步；抓 jelly like capsule（果冻状卵囊）、form an integral part（成为组成部分）。
All the while, the algae are photosynthesizing, transforming sunlight into usable energy, a process that releases oxygen.
自始至终，藻类都在进行光合作用，把阳光转化为可用的能量，这一过程会释放氧气。
🔑 时间伴随信号 All the while；抓 photosynthesizing（光合作用）、releases oxygen（释放氧气）。
This is critical because the embryos need oxygen, and pond water tends to be low in it.
这一点至关重要，因为胚胎需要氧气，而池塘水往往含氧量很低。
🔑 因果信号 because；抓 embryos need oxygen（胚胎需要氧气）。
And in return, the algae get a nutrient that they need to survive: nitrogen.
作为回报，藻类获得了一种它们生存所需的养分：氮。
🔑 互换/回报信号 in return；抓氮元素 nitrogen，是藻类得到的回报。
The embryos produce readily available nitrogen as a waste product, and the algae happily use it.
胚胎作为一种代谢废物，产生了现成可用的氮，而藻类乐意地加以利用。
🔑 抓 waste product（代谢废物），点明氮的来源。
And what's cool is we now know that the algae don't just live in the egg capsule; they can enter the embryos cells, meaning photosynthesis is occurring inside a vertebrate, a highly advanced animal, a phenomenon almost unheard of.
而最酷的是，我们现在知道藻类不仅仅生活在卵囊里；它们还能进入胚胎的细胞，也就是说，光合作用正在脊椎动物——一种高度进化的动物——体内进行，这几乎是一个闻所未闻的现象。
🔑 亮点信号 what's cool is；抓 enter the embryos cells（进入胚胎细胞）、inside a vertebrate（脊椎动物体内）。
Corals and a few other marine invertebrates do this, but among vertebrates it is extremely rare.
珊瑚和一些其他海洋无脊椎动物会这么做，但在脊椎动物中，这种情况极其罕见。
🔑 转折信号 but；抓 extremely rare（极其罕见），强调这一现象的独特性。
🔴 你的漏洞：主旨+细节全崩：remarkable case 主旨没抓住、it 指代(oxygen)、vertebrate/invertebrate 术语没听出来
🛠 补强：学术术语要背：symbiosis共生/photosynthesis光合作用/vertebrate脊椎动物/invertebrate无脊椎动物/amphibian两栖动物/algae藻类/nitrogen氮/oxygen氧。这些词听不懂整篇崩
✓[2026-08-28] Role of Kinship · 亲缘关系（5/10 ❌错5）5/10
📖 原文 · 点任意词展开满配卡 · 🔊 读全文
The role of kinship was central to the social structure of prehistoric communities. Kinship not only structured family relationships but also shaped how resources were shared, labor was organized, and social hierarchies were maintained within the group. Archaeological evidence suggests that individuals held specific roles based on age and gender, contributing to a sophisticated division of labor. The intricacy of these social structures is further evidenced by the existence of ceremonial sites, which indicate collective activities and social gatherings.
🇨🇳 全文翻译（先自己读，点开中英对照）
亲缘关系在史前社群的社会结构中居于核心地位。亲缘关系不仅建构了家庭关系，还塑造了群体内部资源如何共享、劳动如何组织以及社会等级如何维系。考古证据表明，个体依据年龄与性别承担特定角色，促成了精细的劳动分工。这些社会结构的复杂性还进一步体现于礼仪场所的存在，礼仪场所表明当时存在集体活动与社会集会。
🔬 长难句精拆 · 点彩块看成分 · 点词展开卡
主语谓语宾语从句介词/修饰状语
Kinshipnot only structuredfamily relationshipsbut also shapedhow @S{resourceswere shared, laborwas organized, and social hierarchieswere maintainedwithin the group}
先自己拆，再点开看直译 + 读法
直译：亲缘关系不仅建构了家庭关系，也塑造了资源如何被共享、劳动如何被组织、以及社会等级如何在群体内部被维系。
🧭 怎么读懂：先抓主干：Kinship not only structured… but also shaped…（亲缘关系既建构……又塑造……）。not only…but also… 是并列结构，带出两个并列谓语 structured 和 shaped；structured 的宾语是 family relationships。shaped 后接 how 引导的宾语从句（作 shaped 的宾语），从句内部是三个并列的被动小句：resources were shared / labor was organized / social hierarchies were maintained，句末 within the group 是地点状语修饰整个从句。
The intricacy of these social structuresis further evidencedby the existence of ceremonial sites, which indicate collective activities and social gatherings
先自己拆，再点开看直译 + 读法
直译：这些社会结构的复杂性进一步被礼仪场所的存在所证明，这些场所表明存在集体活动与社会集会。
🧭 怎么读懂：主干 The intricacy… is further evidenced（这种复杂性……得到进一步证明），is evidenced 是被动语态（被……证明）。主语里的 of these social structures 是介词短语作定语，修饰 intricacy。by the existence of ceremonial sites 是介词短语，指出证明的施动来源（被什么证明）。句末 which 引导非限制性定语从句，补充说明 ceremonial sites（这些场所表明了集体活动与社会集会）。
🖊 逐空对照 · ✍️ 正确答案已遮住，先回忆再点开核对
#你写的正确考点
1Arc___✗ Archaeological拼写错：Archaeological=archae(古)+ology(学科)+ical。前缀 archaeo 是「古老」
2sp___✗ specific拼写错：specific(特定的)=spec(看)+ific。词根 spec=看。只写了 sp 开头
3a__✗ age基础词拼错：age(年龄)。3 字母都拼不出，是状态问题
4g_____✗ gender拼写错：gender(性别)。词根 gen(出生)。只写了 g 开头
5on✗ of近形词听混：of(的) 听成 on(在…上)
6✓ suggests
7✓ individuals
8✓ based
9✓ to
10✓ sophisticated
🔴 你的漏洞：5错3类：①考古难词Archaeological ②基础词拼不出(age/gender/specific只写开头) ③介词听混(of→on)
🛠 补强：Archaeological=archae(古)+ology+ical；specific=spec(看)；gender=gen(生)。age 这词不该错——拼写要每天手写练
✓[2026-08-28] Ocean Currents · 洋流（7/10 ❌错3）7/10
📖 原文 · 点任意词展开满配卡 · 🔊 读全文
Ocean currents are large-scale movements of seawater that transport heat and nutrients throughout the world's oceans. They are influenced by factors such as wind patterns and Earth's rotation, and they play a crucial role in regulating global climate, since they redistribute heat from warmer regions to cooler ones. This redistribution of heat affects weather systems and contributes to the global water cycle, including evaporation, condensation, and precipitation.
🇨🇳 全文翻译（先自己读，点开中英对照）
洋流是海水的大规模运动，将热量与营养物质输送到世界各大洋各处。它们受风型、地球自转等因素的影响，并在调节全球气候方面发挥至关重要的作用，因为它们把热量从较暖地区重新分配到较冷地区。这种热量的再分配影响天气系统，并参与全球水循环，包括蒸发、凝结与降水。
🔬 长难句精拆 · 点彩块看成分 · 点词展开卡
主语谓语宾语从句介词/修饰状语
Theyare influencedby factors such as wind patterns and Earth's rotation, and theyplaya crucial rolein regulating global climate, since @S{theyredistributeheatfrom warmer regions to cooler ones}
先自己拆，再点开看直译 + 读法
直译：它们被诸如风型与地球自转等因素所影响，并且它们在调节全球气候方面发挥至关重要的作用，因为它们把热量从较暖地区重新分配到较冷地区。
🧭 怎么读懂：整句由 and 连接两个并列分句。分句一是被动结构 They are influenced（它们被影响），by factors 是施动来源，such as 举例说明 factors 是哪些因素。分句二主语 they（前面的 and 是并列连词），谓语 play，宾语 a crucial role，in regulating global climate 是介词短语说明在什么方面发挥关键作用。句末 since 引导原因状语从句（因为……），从句内部 they 为主语、redistribute 为谓语、heat 为宾语、from warmer regions to cooler ones 是介词短语说明从何处到何处。
🖊 逐空对照 · ✍️ 正确答案已遮住，先回忆再点开核对
#你写的正确考点
1fac____✗ factors拼写错：factors(因素)=fact(做)+or。词根 fact=做(同 factory)。只写了 fac
2globle✗ global拼写错：global(全球的)=globe(球)+al。写成 globle，-al 错成 -le
3redist____✗ redistribute拼写错：redistribute=re(再)+distribute(分配)。distribute 没拼全
4✓ influenced
5✓ as
6✓ patterns
7✓ rotation
8✓ crucial
9✓ in
10✓ since
🔴 你的漏洞：3错都是拼写不全：factors/global/redistribute 只写开头几字母
🛠 补强：global=globe+al；factors=fact(做)+or；redistribute=re+distribute。长词拆词根拼
✓[2026-08-28] Museum History · 博物馆（7/10 ❌错3）7/10
📖 原文 · 点任意词展开满配卡 · 🔊 读全文
Museums play a crucial role in preserving and showcasing art, history, and culture. They provide a space where people can engage with multiple forms of artistic expression and historical artifacts. Many museums also offer educational programs and workshops, fostering a deeper understanding of the subjects they display. In addition, digital innovations have enabled museums to reach global audiences through virtual tours and online collections. The patronage of visual arts by individuals and organizations helps support artists and ensures the continued growth and diversity of the cultural landscape.
🇨🇳 全文翻译（先自己读，点开中英对照）
博物馆在保存和展示艺术、历史与文化方面发挥着至关重要的作用。它们为人们提供了一个可以接触多种艺术表现形式和历史文物的空间。许多博物馆还提供教育项目和研习班，从而加深人们对它们所展出主题的理解。此外，数字化创新使博物馆能够通过虚拟导览和在线馆藏接触到全球观众。个人和机构对视觉艺术的资助有助于支持艺术家，并确保文化景观的持续成长与多样性。
🔬 长难句精拆 · 点彩块看成分 · 点词展开卡
主语谓语宾语从句介词/修饰状语
The patronage of visual arts by individuals and organizationshelps supportartistsand ensuresthe continued growth and diversity of the cultural landscape
先自己拆，再点开看直译 + 读法
直译：个人和机构对视觉艺术的资助有助于支持艺术家，并确保文化景观的持续成长与多样性。
🧭 怎么读懂：主干是 The patronage helps support artists and ensures the growth and diversity。主语很长：of visual arts 说明资助的对象，by individuals and organizations 表明资助的主体。谓语是两个并列结构 helps support（帮助支持）和 ensures（确保），各带一个宾语。先锁定主语和两个并列谓语。
🖊 逐空对照 · ✍️ 正确答案已遮住，先回忆再点开核对
#你写的正确考点
1multical✗ multiple拼写错：multiple(多个)=multi(多)+ple。你写 multical，后缀 -ple 错成 -cal。记 multi-PLE
2expresions✗ expression拼写错：expression(表达)=express+ion。你写 expresions，少了一个 s。记 ex-PRESS-ion，双 s
3al__✗ also拼写/漏：also(也)。你写 al__ 没写完。这个不该错，纯手滑
4✓ provide
5✓ space
6✓ people
7✓ engage
8✓ of
9✓ historical
10✓ Many
🔴 你的漏洞：3错：multiple后缀错、expression漏s、also手滑没写完
🛠 补强：后缀 -ple/-ion 串背：multiple/triple, expression/impression。also 这种基础词别再手滑
✓[2026-08-28] Mulberry Craze · 美国桑树狂热（1/4 ❌错3）1/4⏱ 3m50s
🎧 听力原文 · 点词展开 ·
Earlier in class, we talked about the Dutch tulip craze of the early 1600s, a time when tulip bulbs became luxury symbols, prices soared, and fortunes vanished almost overnight. It was one of history's first economic bubbles, fueled by speculation and status. America had its own version in the early 19th century when the country was swept up in the mulberry craze. The idea sounded simple. Plant mulberry trees, raise the silkworms that eat their leaves, collect the silk from the worm's cocoons, and rake in the profits. Silk was a prized luxury import and producing it locally promised enormous wealth. Farmers and investors rushed to buy mulberry saplings. Prices for the young trees soared from pennies to several dollars. Classic bubble behavior. And the speculation wasn't all about money. Silk itself, the idea of silk, carried powerful symbolism: refinement, status, and aspiration. For a brief moment, middle class Americans imagined silk dresses as attainable, blurring old class distinctions. But reality intervened. Silkworms are delicate, requiring precise care and stable climates. Harsh winters wiped out silkworm populations across the country. Mulberry groves lost their purpose. The trees lost their value. The bubble burst, and by the mid 1840s, folks who'd invested their fortunes in the venture were ruined financially. And the bottom line? I mean, for us today? Well, just like tulip mania in the Netherlands, the American mulberry craze shows how ambition and hype can outpace ecological and practical limits. This is a cautionary tale for modern day investment booms.
🇨🇳 听力原文翻译（先自己读，点开中英对照）
早些时候在课上，我们讲过17世纪早期的荷兰郁金香热潮，那是一个郁金香球茎成为奢侈品象征、价格飙升、财富几乎一夜之间蒸发殆尽的时期。那是历史上最早的经济泡沫之一，由投机和身份地位所驱动。美国在19世纪早期也有自己的版本，当时整个国家都陷入了桑树热潮。这个想法听起来很简单。种下桑树，饲养吃桑叶的蚕，从蚕茧中抽取蚕丝，然后大把获利。丝绸是珍贵的奢侈进口品，在当地生产它意味着巨大的财富。农民和投资者争先恐后地购买桑树苗。这些小树苗的价格从几便士飙升到几美元。典型的泡沫行为。而且这种投机并不全是冲着钱去的。丝绸本身、丝绸所代表的理念，承载着强大的象征意义：精致、地位和抱负。在短短的一段时间里，中产阶级美国人想象着丝绸裙子变得触手可及，模糊了旧的阶级界限。但现实介入了。蚕很娇弱，需要精细的照料和稳定的气候。严冬把全国各地的蚕群一扫而空。桑树林失去了用途。桑树失去了价值。泡沫破裂了，到19世纪40年代中期，那些把身家性命投进这项事业的人彻底破产了。那么结论是什么呢？我是说，对我们今天而言？嗯，就像荷兰的郁金香狂热一样，美国的桑树热潮展示了雄心与炒作如何超越生态和现实的限制。这是给现代投资热潮的一个警示故事。
🔬 长难句精拆 · 点彩块看成分 · 点词展开卡
主语谓语宾语从句介词/修饰状语
Earlier in class, we talked about the Dutch tulip craze of the early 1600s, a time when tulip bulbs became luxury symbols, prices soared, and fortunes vanished almost overnight.
先自己拆，再点开看直译 + 读法
直译：早些时候在课上，我们讲到了17世纪早期的荷兰郁金香热潮，那是一个郁金香球茎成为奢侈品象征、价格飙升、财富几乎一夜之间蒸发殆尽的时期。
🧭 怎么读懂：先找主干 we talked about the Dutch tulip craze；Earlier in class 是时间状语。逗号后 a time when... 是 the Dutch tulip craze 的同位语，when 引导的定语从句里并列了三个谓语 became/soared/vanished，分别讲『成为象征、价格飙升、财富蒸发』三个后果，是当年疯狂的具象展开。
Well, just like tulip mania in the Netherlands, the American mulberry craze shows how ambition and hype can outpace ecological and practical limits.
先自己拆，再点开看直译 + 读法
直译：嗯，就像荷兰的郁金香狂热一样，美国的桑树热潮展示了雄心与炒作如何超越生态和现实的限制。
🧭 怎么读懂：主干是 the American mulberry craze shows...。开头的 just like tulip mania 是类比状语，把美国桑树热潮与荷兰郁金香狂热挂钩。shows 后面 how 引导宾语从句，从句里主语 ambition and hype、谓语 outpace、宾语 ecological and practical limits。整句点题：雄心与炒作跑赢了生态和现实限制。
主旨题What is the main focus of the talk?（讲座主旨） ✅ 你答对了

B. The economic history of the American mulberry craze 美国桑树狂热的经济史
C. The life cycle of silkworms 蚕的生命周期
D. Differences between Dutch and American economies 荷兰与美国经济差异

C. The life cycle of silkworms 蚕的生命周期

🧠 推理主旨题：整篇讲 mulberry craze 的经济泡沫故事（买树→涨价→崩盘）→ B

细节题Why does the speaker mention the value of silk as an import?（为何提丝绸进口价值） ❌ 你答错了
A. To indicate why there was relatively little silk in the US 说明美国丝绸少
B. To emphasize that luxury goods often lose value 强调奢侈品常贬值
C. To explain the motivation behind purchasing mulberry trees 解释买桑树的动机
D. To highlight the growing prosperity in the US 强调美国繁荣
🙈 先自己判断，再点开看答案 & 老师精讲

A. To indicate why there was relatively little silk in the US 说明美国丝绸少
B. To emphasize that luxury goods often lose value 强调奢侈品常贬值 ✗ 你选的
C. To explain the motivation behind purchasing mulberry trees 解释买桑树的动机 ✓ 正确
D. To highlight the growing prosperity in the US 强调美国繁荣
📍 定位'Silk was a prized luxury import and producing it locally promised enormous wealth'（丝绸是珍贵进口，本地生产承诺巨大财富）
🧠 推理修辞目的题：提 silk 是珍贵进口 → 本地生产能致富 → 所以人们买桑树。动机(motivation) → C
✂️ 排除B(奢侈品贬值)不是提 silk 价值的目的；A/D 原文没提

A. Insect pests damaged most of the trees 虫害
B. Economic difficulties reduced demand for silk 经济困难减少需求

📝 关键句 · 点词展开卡 · 学表达
Earlier in class, we talked about the Dutch tulip craze of the early 1600s, a time when tulip bulbs became luxury symbols, prices soared, and fortunes vanished almost overnight.
早些时候在课上，我们讲到了17世纪早期的荷兰郁金香热潮，那是一个郁金香球茎成为奢侈品象征、价格飙升、财富几乎一夜之间蒸发殆尽的时期。
🔑 信号词 Earlier in class 引出对比/铺垫对象；抓实词 tulip craze（郁金香热潮）作为引子，注意时间 1600s。
It was one of history's first economic bubbles, fueled by speculation and status.
那是历史上最早的经济泡沫之一，由投机和身份地位所驱动。
🔑 下定义句；抓 economic bubbles（经济泡沫）、speculation、status 三个关键词，奠定全篇『泡沫』主题。
America had its own version in the early 19th century when the country was swept up in the mulberry craze.
美国在19世纪早期也有自己的版本，当时整个国家都陷入了桑树热潮。
🔑 类比/过渡信号 own version；抓主题词 mulberry craze（桑树热潮），这是本篇核心对象。
The idea sounded simple.
这个想法听起来很简单。
🔑 过渡句；simple 为后文 But reality intervened 埋伏笔，注意保留对『简单』的预期。
Plant mulberry trees, raise the silkworms that eat their leaves, collect the silk from the worm's cocoons, and rake in the profits.
种下桑树，饲养吃桑叶的蚕，从蚕茧中抽取蚕丝，然后大把获利。
🔑 步骤并列列举；抓动词链 Plant/raise/collect/rake in，听清四个环节即可。
Silk was a prized luxury import and producing it locally promised enormous wealth.
丝绸是珍贵的奢侈进口品，在当地生产它意味着巨大的财富。
🔑 因果动机句；抓 prized luxury import（珍贵奢侈进口品）和 enormous wealth（巨额财富）。
Farmers and investors rushed to buy mulberry saplings.
农民和投资者争先恐后地购买桑树苗。
🔑 投机行为描写；抓 rushed（争抢）、saplings（树苗）。
Prices for the young trees soared from pennies to several dollars.
这些小树苗的价格从几便士飙升到几美元。
🔑 数字对比信号；抓价格跨度 from pennies to several dollars，是泡沫典型标志。
Classic bubble behavior.
典型的泡沫行为。
🔑 下结论短句；抓 bubble（泡沫），呼应第二句的经济泡沫。
And the speculation wasn't all about money.
而且这种投机并不全是冲着钱去的。
🔑 递进信号 And；语意转折，引出下面关于象征意义的内容。
Silk itself, the idea of silk, carried powerful symbolism: refinement, status, and aspiration.
丝绸本身、丝绸所代表的理念，承载着强大的象征意义：精致、地位和抱负。
🔑 冒号列举；抓三个并列词 refinement/status/aspiration，听清象征意义。
For a brief moment, middle class Americans imagined silk dresses as attainable, blurring old class distinctions.
在短短的一段时间里，中产阶级美国人想象着丝绸裙子变得触手可及，模糊了旧的阶级界限。
🔑 时间限定 For a brief moment；抓 attainable（可触及）、blurring class distinctions（模糊阶级界限）。
But reality intervened.
但现实介入了。
🔑 强转折信号词 But，全篇结构分水岭：由狂热转向破灭。
Silkworms are delicate, requiring precise care and stable climates.
蚕很娇弱，需要精细的照料和稳定的气候。
🔑 原因解释句；抓 delicate（娇弱）、precise care and stable climates。
Harsh winters wiped out silkworm populations across the country.
严冬把全国各地的蚕群一扫而空。
🔑 因果落点；抓 wiped out（消灭殆尽），是泡沫破裂的直接原因。
Mulberry groves lost their purpose.
桑树林失去了用途。
🔑 连锁结果；抓 lost their purpose。
The trees lost their value.
桑树失去了价值。
🔑 并列强调，与上句构成递进；抓 lost their value。
The bubble burst, and by the mid 1840s, folks who'd invested their fortunes in the venture were ruined financially.
泡沫破裂了，到19世纪40年代中期，那些把身家性命投进这项事业的人彻底破产了。
🔑 结果句；抓 bubble burst（泡沫破裂）、ruined financially（破产），时间 mid 1840s。
And the bottom line? I mean, for us today?
那么结论是什么呢？我是说，对我们今天而言？
🔑 总结提问信号 bottom line（结论）；自问自答引出下文主旨。
Well, just like tulip mania in the Netherlands, the American mulberry craze shows how ambition and hype can outpace ecological and practical limits.
嗯，就像荷兰的郁金香狂热一样，美国的桑树热潮展示了雄心与炒作如何超越生态和现实的限制。
🔑 类比信号 just like；抓核心结论 outpace ecological and practical limits（超越生态与现实限制）。
This is a cautionary tale for modern day investment booms.
这是给现代投资热潮的一个警示故事。
🔑 全文主旨句；抓 cautionary tale（警示故事），是讲座的落点与考点。
🔴 你的漏洞：3错全是细节/态度：修辞目的(为何提silk)、因果(为何失去价值)、结尾态度(cautionary tale)都没抓住
🛠 补强：学术讲座抓信号词：But reality intervened(转折，后面是关键)、Well(总结)、cautionary tale(态度)。细节题考『为什么』，听 but/because/so 后面的因果
✓[2026-08-28] Moon Phases in History · 月相（10/10 ✅）10/10
📖 原文 · 点任意词展开满配卡 · 🔊 读全文
Observing the moon's phases has been important for many cultures throughout history, influencing calendars, navigation, and mythology. Lunar cycles formed the basis of early timekeeping systems, such as the Islamic Hijri calendar and the traditional Chinese lunisolar calendar. Seafarers used the position of the moon to make predictions about ocean levels and its illumination to guide their voyages, especially during night travel. In mythology, the moon often embodied powerful deities or symbols, shaping rituals, stories, and seasonal festivals. Across continents, the waxing and waning of the moon has remained a universal clock and an inspiration for human imagination.
🇨🇳 全文翻译（先自己读，点开中英对照）
纵观历史，观察月相一直对许多文化都至关重要，它影响着历法、航海和神话。月亮的盈亏周期构成了早期计时系统的基础，例如伊斯兰希吉来历和传统的中国阴阳合历。航海者利用月亮的位置来预测海平面的涨落，并借助月光为航行导航，尤其是在夜间航行时。在神话中，月亮往往象征着强大的神灵或符号，塑造着各种仪式、故事和季节性节日。在各大洲，月亮的盈亏始终是一座普世的时钟，也是人类想象力的灵感源泉。
🔬 长难句精拆 · 点彩块看成分 · 点词展开卡
主语谓语宾语从句介词/修饰状语
Seafarers used the position of the moon to make predictions about ocean levels and its illumination to guide their voyages, especially during night travel.
先自己拆，再点开看直译 + 读法
直译：航海者们利用月亮的位置来做出关于海平面（涨落）的预测，并利用它的光亮来引导他们的航行，尤其是在夜间旅行时。
🧭 怎么读懂：先抓主干 Seafarers used A and B（航海者利用A和B）；谓语 used 后面用 and 并列了两个宾语——「月亮的位置」和「它的光亮」，各自又带一个 to do 不定式作目的状语（做海平面预测 / 为航行导航）；最后逗号后的 especially during night travel 是补充说明的时间状语，整句是说航海者用月相和月光分别完成两件事。
🖊 逐空对照 · ✍️ 正确答案已遮住，先回忆再点开核对
#你写的正确考点
1✓ used
2✓ position
3✓ the
4✓ to
5✓ predictions
6✓ ocean
7✓ and
8✓ illumination
9✓ guide
10✓ voyages
🔴 你的漏洞：这篇 10/10，无需纠错
🛠 补强：生词照背：phases/lunar/lunisolar/seafarers/illumination/deities/waxing/waning 都是天文历史高频词，进词库
✓[2026-08-28] Medieval Town Origins · 中世纪城镇起源（题3-5）见总览
📖 原文 · 点任意词展开满配卡 · 🔊 读全文
Two manifestations of the vitality of the Late Middle Ages were the rise of towns and the development of a new business and commercial class in Europe. Early medieval society had been traditional, agricultural, and rural, so the appearance of a new class that was none of these constituted a social revolution. The new class—artisans and merchants—came from the peasantry. They were landless younger sons of large families, driven away by land shortage. Or they were forced by war and famine to seek new possibilities.
Historians have proposed three basic theories to explain the origins of European towns. Some scholars believe towns began as boroughs—that is, as fortifications erected during the ninth-century Viking invasions. Belgian historian Henri Pirenne maintained that towns sprang up when merchants who engaged in long-distance trade gravitated toward favorable spots, such as a fort. A third explanation focuses on religious establishments such as monasteries and the great cathedrals, which represented a demand for goods and services. Traders and merchants settled near religious establishments to cater to the residents' economic needs.
All three theories have some validity, though none of them explains the origins of all medieval towns. Some medieval towns that became flourishing centers of trade by the mid-twelfth century had originally been Roman army camps. Pirenne's interpretation accurately describes the Flemish towns of Gent, Bruges, and Ypres but does not fit the course of development in the Italian cities or in such centers as London. Nor does the Pirenne thesis take into account the significance of local trade and markets in the growth of towns.
🇨🇳 全文翻译（先自己读，点开中英对照）
中世纪晚期活力的两种表现，是欧洲城镇的兴起以及一个新的商业阶级的发展。早期中世纪社会一直是传统的、农业的、乡村的，因此一个与这些特征都不沾边的新阶级的出现，构成了一场社会革命。这个新阶级——工匠和商人——来自农民阶层。他们是大家庭中没有土地的幼子，被土地短缺所驱离。或者他们是被战争和饥荒逼迫去寻找新的可能性。 历史学家提出了三种基本理论来解释欧洲城镇的起源。一些学者认为，城镇始于“borough”——即第九世纪维京人入侵期间修建的防御工事。比利时历史学家亨利·皮雷纳主张，当从事长途贸易的商人被吸引到有利地点（如堡垒）时，城镇便兴起了。第三种解释聚焦于宗教机构，如修道院和大教堂，它们代表着对商品和服务的需求。商贩和商人在宗教机构附近定居，以满足居民的经济需求。 三种理论都有一定道理，尽管没有一种能解释所有中世纪城镇的起源。一些到十二世纪中叶已成为繁荣贸易中心的中世纪城镇，原本是罗马军队的营地。皮雷纳的解释准确描述了佛兰德斯的根特、布鲁日和伊珀尔等城镇，但并不符合意大利城市或伦敦等中心的发展历程。皮雷纳的理论也没有考虑到地方贸易和市场在城镇发展中的重要性。
🔬 长难句精拆 · 点彩块看成分 · 点词展开卡
主语谓语宾语从句介词/修饰状语
Early medieval society had been traditional, agricultural, and rural, so @S{the appearance of a new class @C{that was none of these} constituted a social revolution}.
先自己拆，再点开看直译 + 读法
直译：早期中世纪社会一直是传统的、农业的、乡村的，因此一个与这些特征都不沾边的新阶级的出现，构成了一场社会革命。
🧭 怎么读懂：前半句主干 Early medieval society had been traditional, agricultural, and rural（早期中世纪社会一直是传统的、农业的、乡村的）；so 引导结果状语从句，从句主干是 the appearance of a new class ... constituted a social revolution（新阶级的出现构成了一场社会革命）；that was none of these 是定语从句修饰 a new class，these 指代前面三个形容词 traditional、agricultural、rural。
Belgian historian Henri Pirenne maintained that @S{towns sprang up when @S{merchants @C{who engaged in long-distance trade} gravitated toward favorable spots, such as a fort}}.
先自己拆，再点开看直译 + 读法
直译：比利时历史学家亨利·皮雷纳主张，当从事长途贸易的商人被吸引到有利地点（如堡垒）时，城镇便兴起了。
🧭 怎么读懂：先抓主干 Henri Pirenne maintained that ...（皮雷纳主张……）；that 引导宾语从句，其主干是 towns sprang up（城镇兴起）；when 引导时间状语从句，从句主干是 merchants ... gravitated toward favorable spots（商人被吸引到有利地点）；who engaged in long-distance trade 是定语从句修饰 merchants；such as a fort 举例说明 favorable spots 指什么。

✓[2026-08-28] Growth Mindset in Education · 成长型思维（5/5 ✅）5/5
📖 原文 · 点任意词展开满配卡 · 🔊 读全文
The concept of a growth mindset has gained attention in educational psychology. Coined by Carol Dweck, the phrase "growth mindset" refers to the belief that abilities and intelligence can be developed through dedication and hard work. This contrasts with a fixed mindset, where individuals believe their abilities are static.
Research suggests that students with a growth mindset are more likely to embrace challenges, persevere through difficulties, and see effort as a path to mastery. For example, a student who struggles with math but believes they can improve with practice is displaying a growth mindset. This belief encourages resilience and a positive attitude towards learning.
Educators play a crucial role in fostering a growth mindset in their students. Techniques such as praising effort rather than innate ability and providing constructive feedback can help students develop this mindset. Creating a classroom environment that celebrates mistakes as learning opportunities can reinforce the growth mindset philosophy.
[A] However, implementing growth mindset strategies is not without challenges. [B] Some students may find it difficult to change how they view their abilities, especially if they have been accustomed to a fixed mindset for a long time. [C] Additionally, educators themselves must genuinely believe in the growth mindset principles to effectively convey them to their students. [D]
🇨🇳 全文翻译（先自己读，点开中英对照）
成长型思维这一概念在教育心理学中日益受到关注。由卡罗尔·德韦克提出的“成长型思维”一词，指的是一种信念，即能力和智力可以通过专注投入与勤奋努力得到发展。这与固定型思维形成对比，在固定型思维下，人们认为自己的能力是一成不变的。 研究表明，拥有成长型思维的学生更愿意迎接挑战、在困难中坚持不懈，并把努力视为通往精通的途径。例如，一个在数学上感到吃力、却相信自己通过练习能够进步的学生，正是在展现成长型思维。这种信念能培养韧性以及对学习的积极态度。 教育者在培养学生成长型思维方面起着至关重要的作用。诸如表扬努力而非天赋、提供建设性反馈等技巧，可以帮助学生养成这种思维。营造一个把错误当作学习机会来对待的课堂环境，能够强化成长型思维的理念。 然而，落实成长型思维策略并非没有挑战。有些学生可能难以改变他们看待自身能力的方式，尤其是当他们长期以来已习惯固定型思维时。此外，教育者自己必须真正相信成长型思维的原则，才能有效地把这些原则传递给学生。
🔬 长难句精拆 · 点彩块看成分 · 点词展开卡
主语谓语宾语从句介词/修饰状语
Coined by Carol Dweck, the phrase "growth mindset" refers to the belief @C{that @S{abilities and intelligence can be developed through dedication and hard work}}.
先自己拆，再点开看直译 + 读法
直译：由卡罗尔·德韦克所创，“成长型思维”这一短语指的是一种信念，即能力和智力可以通过专注投入与勤奋努力而被发展。
🧭 怎么读懂：先抓主干 the phrase "growth mindset" refers to the belief（“成长型思维”这一短语指的是一种信念）；句首 Coined by Carol Dweck 是过去分词短语作状语，修饰 the phrase，说明这个词是谁提出的；that 引导同位语从句，解释 belief 的具体内容，从句内部主干是 abilities and intelligence can be developed（能力和智力能被发展），through dedication and hard work 是方式状语。
For example, a student @C{who @V{struggles with math but believes they can improve @P{with practice}}} is displaying a growth mindset.
先自己拆，再点开看直译 + 读法
直译：例如，一个在数学上感到吃力、却相信自己通过练习能够进步的学生，正在展现一种成长型思维。
🧭 怎么读懂：先找主干 a student ... is displaying a growth mindset（一个学生正在展现成长型思维）；who 引导定语从句修饰 a student，从句内部用 but 并列两个谓语：struggles with math（在数学上吃力）与 believes（相信）；believes 之后又接了一个省略 that 的宾语从句 they can improve，with practice 是其中的方式状语。
词汇题The word "persevere" in the passage is closest in meaning to（persevere 最接近） ✅ 你答对了
A. persist 坚持
B. change 改变
C. hesitate 犹豫
D. succeed 成功
🙈 先自己判断，再点开看答案 & 老师精讲
A. persist 坚持 ✓ 正确
B. change 改变
C. hesitate 犹豫
D. succeed 成功
📍 定位'persevere through difficulties'（在困难中…）
🧠 推理词汇题靠上下文：前文 embrace challenges（拥抱挑战）、后文 see effort as path to mastery，都在说『坚持不放弃』→ persevere = persist
✂️ 排除change/hesitate 与『克服困难』语义相反；succeed 是结果不是过程
细节题Which is true about students with a growth mindset?（成长型思维的学生） ✅ 你答对了
A. They believe intelligence is unchangeable 智力不可变
B. They avoid challenges 逃避挑战
C. They view effort as a means to improve 视努力为提升途径
D. They are naturally talented 天生有才

C. insufficient research 研究不足

📍 定位'Some students may find it difficult to change how they view their abilities'（学生难改变对自己能力的看法）

✂️ 排除B/C/D 原文都没提；陷阱是 D(教师)在原文是『教师自己也要信』，不是『负担重』
🔴 你的漏洞：5/5 全对，词汇题/EXCEPT/因果题的上下文推理都在线

✓[2026-08-28] Green Roof Benefits · 绿色屋顶（3/5 ❌错2）3/5
📖 原文 · 点任意词展开满配卡 · 🔊 读全文
Green roofs, or living roofs, are gaining prominence in urban areas due to their environmental benefits. These are roofs covered with vegetation to help manage stormwater by absorbing rainfall, decreasing runoff, and minimizing flooding risks. Another advantage of green roofs is their ability to improve air quality. Plants on the roof filter pollutants and capture dust. Additionally, green roofs reduce the urban heat island effect. Conventional roofs absorb and radiate heat, increasing urban temperatures. In contrast, green roofs keep buildings cooler by providing insulation and emitting moisture through their plants and soil.
Green roofs offer energy-efficiency benefits. The insulation provided by soil and plants reduces the need for heating in winter and cooling in summer, leading to energy savings. Green roofs can also extend the lifespan of the roof by shielding materials from UV radiation and extreme temperatures, keeping the underlying structure sound for longer.
Finally, green roofs support urban biodiversity. Their vegetative layers create habitats for insects and birds in cities where green spaces are limited. These microhabitats help sustain pollinator populations and promote ecological balance. [A] Green roofs can also grow food, contributing to food security in urban environments. [B] Despite these advantages, green roofs can be costly to install. [C] However, many cities offer incentives to encourage their adoption. [D]
🇨🇳 全文翻译（先自己读，点开中英对照）
绿色屋顶，又称“活屋顶”，正因其环境效益而在城市地区日益受到重视。这些是覆盖植被的屋顶，通过吸收雨水、减少径流、降低洪涝风险来帮助管理雨水径流。绿色屋顶的另一优势在于改善空气质量的能力。屋顶上的植物能过滤污染物并吸附灰尘。此外，绿色屋顶还能减轻城市热岛效应。传统屋顶吸收并散发热量，推高城市温度。相比之下，绿色屋顶通过提供隔热，并通过其植物与土壤散湿，使建筑保持更凉爽。绿色屋顶具有能效优势。土壤与植物提供的隔热，降低了冬季供暖与夏季制冷的需求，从而节省能源。绿色屋顶还能通过使材料免受紫外线辐射与极端温度的影响，延长屋顶的使用寿命，使底层结构更长久地保持完好。最后，绿色屋顶支持城市生物多样性。其植被层在绿地有限的城市中为昆虫与鸟类创造栖息地。这些微生境有助于维系传粉昆虫种群并促进生态平衡。[A] 绿色屋顶还能种植食物，为城市环境中的粮食安全作出贡献。[B] 尽管有这些优势，绿色屋顶的安装成本可能高昂。[C] 然而，许多城市提供激励措施以鼓励采用绿色屋顶。[D]
🔬 长难句精拆 · 点彩块看成分 · 点词展开卡
主语谓语宾语从句介词/修饰状语
Green roofscan also extendthe lifespan of the roofby shielding materials from UV radiation and extreme temperatures, keeping the underlying structure sound for longer
先自己拆，再点开看直译 + 读法
直译：绿色屋顶也能通过使材料免受紫外线辐射与极端温度的影响来延长屋顶的寿命，从而使底层结构更长久地保持完好。
🧭 怎么读懂：主干 Green roofs can also extend the lifespan of the roof（绿色屋顶也能延长屋顶寿命），can 是情态动词。by shielding materials from UV radiation and extreme temperatures 是介词短语作方式状语，说明“通过什么方式”延长寿命。句末 keeping the underlying structure sound for longer 是现在分词短语作结果/伴随状语，表示延长寿命带来的结果；其中 keep + 宾语 + 形容词（sound）构成使役结构，keep the structure sound 意为“使结构保持完好”。
In contrast, green roofskeepbuildingscoolerby providing insulation and emitting moisturethrough their plants and soil
先自己拆，再点开看直译 + 读法
直译：相比之下，绿色屋顶通过提供隔热并经其植物与土壤散湿，使建筑保持更凉爽。
🧭 怎么读懂：主干 Green roofs keep buildings cooler（绿色屋顶使建筑保持更凉爽），keep 是使役动词，后接宾语 buildings + 宾语补足语 cooler（keep + 宾语 + 形容词）。by providing insulation and emitting moisture 是介词短语作方式状语（通过提供隔热并散湿），其中 providing 与 emitting 是并列的动名词，共同作介词 by 的宾语。through their plants and soil 是介词短语，进一步说明散湿的媒介（经由植物与土壤）。句首 In contrast 是状语，引出与前文传统屋顶的对比。

A. By eliminating the need for heating and air conditioning 免除取暖空调 ✗ 你选的
B. By creating microhabitats for pollinators 为授粉昆虫造微栖息地

✓[2026-08-28] Geological Formations · 地质构造（7/10 ❌错3）7/10
📖 原文 · 点任意词展开满配卡 · 🔊 读全文
The study of geological formations provides insights into Earth's dynamic processes. Metamorphic rocks are rocks that have been transformed by heat and pressure. Sedimentary rocks form as layers of materials build up over time and are eventually pressed and fused together to create solid rock. The strata inside these formations serve as historical records of environmental conditions, revealing changes in climate, sea levels, and biological evolution. Analyzing rocks allows geologists to reconstruct past geological events.
🇨🇳 全文翻译（先自己读，点开中英对照）
对地质构造的研究为人们了解地球的动态过程提供了洞见。变质岩是被热量和压力转化而成的岩石。沉积岩随着物质层层堆积、并最终被压实和熔合在一起，形成坚硬的岩石。这些构造中的地层充当了环境状况的历史记录，揭示了气候、海平面和生物演化方面的变化。分析岩石使地质学家能够重建过去的地质事件。
🔬 长难句精拆 · 点彩块看成分 · 点词展开卡
主语谓语宾语从句介词/修饰状语
The strata inside these formationsserveas historical records of environmental conditions, revealing changes in climate, sea levels, and biological evolution
先自己拆，再点开看直译 + 读法
直译：这些构造中的地层充当了环境状况的历史记录，揭示了气候、海平面和生物演化方面的变化。
🧭 怎么读懂：主干是 The strata serve as historical records（地层充当历史记录）。主语里 inside these formations 是介词短语修饰 strata；serve as 表示“充当、作为”。句尾 revealing... 是现在分词短语作状语，补充说明这些记录揭示的内容，逻辑上相当于“从而揭示了……”。
Sedimentary rocksformas layers of materials build up over timeand are eventually pressed and fused togetherto create solid rock
先自己拆，再点开看直译 + 读法
直译：沉积岩随着物质层层堆积、并最终被压实和熔合在一起，形成坚硬的岩石。
🧭 怎么读懂：主语 Sedimentary rocks 带两个并列谓语：form（形成）和 are pressed and fused（被压实熔合）。as 引导时间/方式状语从句说明沉积岩如何形成；to create solid rock 是不定式表结果。先抓两个并列谓语，再补从句和不定式。
🖊 逐空对照 · ✍️ 正确答案已遮住，先回忆再点开核对
#你写的正确考点
1layout✗ layers近形词听混：layer(层)=lay(铺)+er。你写 layout(布局) 是另一个词。sedimentary rocks form as LAYERS(沉积岩成层)
2preduce✗ pressed拼写错：pressed(被压实)=press+ed。你写 preduce 是乱拼。记 press 压→pressed
3fully✗ fused近形词听混：fused(熔合)=fuse(熔)。你写 fully(完全)。pressed and fused=压实+熔合
4✓ form
5✓ materials
6✓ up
7✓ time
8✓ are
9✓ to
10✓ solid
🔴 你的漏洞：3错2类：①近形词听混(layers→layout, fused→fully)②拼写乱(preduce)。地质词层=layer/strata/fuse 不熟
🛠 补强：地质主题词串背：layer层/strata地层/fuse熔合/press压实/sedimentary沉积/metamorphic变质。近形词成对记：layer-layout, fuse-fully

✓[2026-08-28] Economics And Resource Allocation · 经济学（5/10 ❌错5）5/10
📖 原文 · 点任意词展开满配卡 · 🔊 读全文
Economics is the social science that studies the production, distribution, and consumption of goods and services. It examines how individuals, households, businesses, and governments make choices about resource allocation and how such decisions affect overall stability and growth in the economy. Through the application of theoretical models and the collection of empirical data, economists can help policymakers formulate strategies to address issues such as inflation, unemployment, and trade imbalances, thereby improving societal well-being.
🇨🇳 全文翻译（先自己读，点开中英对照）
经济学是一门研究商品和服务的生产、分配与消费的社会科学。它考察个人、家庭、企业和政府如何就资源配置做出选择，以及这些决策如何影响经济的整体稳定与增长。通过运用理论模型和收集经验数据，经济学家能够帮助政策制定者制定策略，以应对通货膨胀、失业和贸易失衡等问题，从而增进社会福祉。
🔬 长难句精拆 · 点彩块看成分 · 点词展开卡
主语谓语宾语从句介词/修饰状语
It examines how @S{individuals, households, businesses, and governments make choices about resource allocation} and how @S{such decisions affect overall stability and growth in the economy}.
先自己拆，再点开看直译 + 读法
直译：它考察个体、家庭、企业和政府如何就资源配置做出选择，以及这些决策如何影响经济中的整体稳定与增长。
🧭 怎么读懂：先抓主干 It examines A and B（它考察A和B）；谓语 examines 后面并列两个由 how 引导的宾语从句，用 and 连接；再分别进入每个 how 从句抓各自的主谓宾——「（四类）主体如何做出选择」与「这些决策如何影响稳定与增长」，两个从句末尾的 about/in 介词短语都是各自宾语的后置修饰。
🖊 逐空对照 · ✍️ 正确答案已遮住，先回忆再点开核对
#你写的正确考点
1examinat✗ examines漏了 -es 结尾：examining→examines，第三·人称单数要加 -s（他/it）。你写成 examinat 连 -es 都没写全
2indivicials✗ individuals拼写中间段错：indi-vi-du-als，你写成 indivicials。记口诀：in-di-vi-du-al（把 vid 想成 video 的『看』）
3businesman✗ businesses既拼错又混词：business（生意）≠ businessman（商人）。复数 businesses 直接 business + es，你把 busi-ness 拼成 busines，又串到 man
4resolves✗ resource近形词听错/看错：resource（资源）= re+source（来源）。你写成 resolves（解决），是听成了 resolve 的变形。resource 结尾 -source 是『源头』
5afford✗ affect近形词混淆：affect（影响，动词）= af+fect。你写成 afford（负担得起）。口诀：affect 影响 effect 结果；afford 是『出得起钱』，两个 a 开头的动词易混
🔴 你的漏洞：5 个空全错，3 类病根：①第三人称单数漏 -s（examines）②长词中段拼错（individuals/businesses）③近形词听混（resource→resolves, affect→afford）
🛠 补强：拼写专项：把 resource/affect/businesses/individuals 这些高频学术词每天手写 3 遍；近形词成对记（resource-resolve, affect-afford-effect）

If you're ok giving up some premium features, these are great for students on a shoestring.
